## THE MISSION — the standard every finding is measured against

RapidTool must be **a truly autonomous software factory / AI agentic harness**: given a brief and a
DeepSeek API-keyed model, it creates software **with GOOD code — not bad code** — and it must be able to
make **any kind of software or thing: games, websites, programs, applications, tools, and more.** It must
**continuously improve / refactor / enhance** what it produces, and the software it produces must have
**perfect UI/UX**. Audit accordingly: a defect is anything — code, wiring, process, template, UI, honesty
surface, or missing capability — that stands between RapidTool and that standard.

---

**A4 LANE (RapidTool's own UI/UX + docs) — read window 2026-09-26 15:35→15:48 local.** `console.tsx`
churned 6369→6537 lines across 5 hashes while W24-U1 edited it; `__root.tsx` churned under W24-U2. Every
anchor below is stamped with its read time; the final verification sweep was 15:48:04, `console.tsx`
md5 `fa6aa7965a71b7c8b1afa1c01d36d2db` (6537 lines). Re-grep anchors before patching. Two candidates were
OBSOLETED mid-audit by sibling fixes (A4-x01/x02, recorded at the end, no action owed).

**A4-f01 · HONESTY (self-description) · P1 ·** Every surface calls RapidTool an "autonomous software agency"
while the product's own truth surface says it is not.
- Evidence (read 15:42–15:48): `src/components/console.tsx:1643` home tagline (`autonomous software agency`,
  md5 `fa6aa79…`); `src/routes/index.tsx:7` meta title "RapidTool — autonomous software agency";
  `src/routes/__root.tsx:41` meta description "RapidTool is an autonomous software agency. …" (md5 `a911139…`).
- Counter-truth: `src/lib/doctor.test.ts:28-30` pins `autonomousSoftwareFactory === false` for RapidTool-now;
  README.md:15-18 "RapidTool is **not yet** an autonomous software factory"; `console.tsx:1421` (in-app Guide,
  section "What RapidTool is"): "It does not build software itself".
- Why vs mission: the mission demands a truly autonomous factory — until `doctor` says true, declare-or-strike
  forbids the claim; it sits on every browser tab + the home header a beginner sees first.
- Fix: reword to a true descriptor (“governed delivery harness — brief → audited 13-phase run → Agent Packet”)
  or render the phrase from the doctor claim. Owners: U1 (console), U2 (routes).
- Verify: `grep -rn "autonomous software agency" src/` → 0 hits; `doctor` keeps printing false.

**A4-f02 · LABEL vs EFFECT · P2 ·** The fixtures loader's honesty label promises an approval no surface implements.
- Evidence (read 15:38 + 15:45): `src/lib/fixtures.server.ts:25-26` LOAD_LABEL "…**approving here records a
  decision**, it does not modify the bar" (same string in `fixtures.review.ts:36` fallback), rendered verbatim at
  `console.tsx:490`; U1's new paragraph at `console.tsx:493-497` says "no approve/reject control is wired yet,
  so nothing here can record a decision". The store has no fixture action (`fixes/263_lane5_ui_console.md` §3
  registers it as owed); the only real flip is CLI `fixtures --approve --ids` (`brief-fixtures.ts:588`
  `approveFixtureCases`, called at `scripts/rapidtool-cli.mjs:983-1000`).
- Why vs mission: same screen asserts two opposite things; a label must describe an effect the code can deliver.
- Fix: rewrite LOAD_LABEL (both files) to the truth — e.g. "read-only review — approval is a CLI action
  (`fixtures --approve --ids …`); this view records no decision". `fixtures.server.ts` is outside U1's file box.
- Verify: reload the Eval-fixtures tab — label and panel paragraph agree; `grep -rn "approving here" src/` → 0.

**A4-f03 · HONESTY/VOCABULARY · P2 ·** The highest-stakes control in the UI names a "sandbox" the docs say does
not exist.
- Evidence (read 15:45–15:48): `console.tsx:3773` override checkbox "I reviewed the failing **sandbox** output and
  approve anyway."; `console.tsx:1503` SHORTCUTS "a failed **sandbox** gate needs the override tick first";
  `console.tsx:1965` the `a`-key error repeats it. Against: `console.tsx:262` in-file rule "NEVER \"sandbox\"
  (E-03/E-21)"; README.md:118-120 "**No real isolation** … it is **not** a sandbox — Doctor says `sandbox: false`,
  and nothing may claim otherwise"; AGENT_GUIDE.md:83. The engine's P4 copy is careful ("Engine-template runner
  (bounded…)") but its stakes line (`engine.ts:801`) still says "Sandbox and authorization boundary".
- Why vs mission: a beginner ticking that box believes an OS sandbox failed; a jargon term appears undefined at
  the exact point a human decision is made.
- Fix: reword the three console strings to "bounded-runner self-test output" (or add `TERMS["runner"]` + `<Term>`),
  and align `engine.ts:801`'s stakes line (A1's lane).
- Verify: `grep -n "sandbox" src/components/console.tsx` → only comments + `project.sandbox` field reads; the
  checkbox reads bounded-runner.

**A4-f04 · DOCS CONTRADICT THE PRODUCT · P1/P2 ·** "It does not build software itself" vs the P6-synthesis +
tree-run story.
- Evidence (read 15:35–15:48): AGENT_GUIDE.md:4-7 + :52-54 ("RapidTool is a deterministic, governed **pre-flight
  compiler**: it does not build software itself… P6 is a bounded *template self-test*… The **real** code is yours
  to write"), reproduced in the in-app Guide `console.tsx:1421`. README.md:71-74 "Synthesis: P6 asks a model for
  module code in signature-bound chunks, then compile-checks and runs each job module's own oracle";
  README.md:95 "tree-run = the product builds and tests ✅".
- Why vs mission: AGENT_GUIDE is the agent's mandatory read and the Guide dialog is the beginner's; both are told
  the tool does not build software while the capability table says it does. Neither reader can tell what the
  P6-synthesized module actually is.
- Fix: ONE reconciled story with a verified census (see A4-f11): e.g. "emits + verifies a real module set through
  P6 synthesis (census: <verified>); breadth beyond the reference corpus is limited by <X>".
- Verify: the corrected sentence's census reproduces via `node scripts/rapidtool-cli.mjs doctor --tree <fresh
  keyless emit>`.

**A4-f05 · DOCS / AGENT ONBOARDING · P2 ·** AGENT_GUIDE says there is no feedback channel; the product ships `ingest`.
- Evidence (read 15:35, md5 `d3a3034…`): AGENT_GUIDE.md:69 "RapidTool has no live LLM/research/sandbox feedback
  channel (client-side, localStorage-only)"; §2 step 4 "Import the resulting report JSON **if the app offers it**"
  — it does: CLI `ingest` (`scripts/rapidtool-cli.mjs:680`; README-cli.md:80-83, :933) and the console's Ingest
  report panel (`console.tsx:2834-2837` toggle, `:2138` `onIngestReport`, textarea ~`:2900-2927`), plus crew
  artifact ingestion (`console.tsx:2158-2182`).
- Why vs mission: the guide is how an agent learns to close the loop; omitting the one verb that does it pushes
  agents to hand-copy into Notes.
- Fix: rewrite AGENT_GUIDE §4 item 1 to name `ingest --report …` (CLI) and the console's Ingest report panel,
  keeping the localStorage caveat.
- Verify: grep AGENT_GUIDE for `ingest` ≥1 hit; the documented path runs keyless.

**A4-f06 · ONBOARDING (beginner) · P3 ·** The first screen offers no help entry and hides the 12-char minimum.
- Evidence (read 15:35 + 15:48): HomeScreen (console.tsx ~1508-1608 at first read) renders the brief box,
  templates and Builds only; `Guide/Settings/Shortcuts` buttons render ONLY in RunScreen's header
  (`setGuideOpen(true)` hits at console.tsx:1983 — `?` shortcut — and :2563 — header button; no Home occurrence).
  Launch is `disabled={brief.trim().length < 12}` (console.tsx:~1676) and `launch()` silently returns under 12
  (:1629) — no visible reason.
- Why vs mission: beginner onboarding must be self-explanatory; a disabled primary button with no hint + help one
  click deeper (after creating a build) is the dead end the Guide exists to prevent.
- Fix: add a "How it works" button on HomeScreen reusing `GuideDialog`; add a hint line under the launch button
  ("briefs need 12+ characters — the button unlocks then").
- Verify: open `/` — help affordance present; type 5 chars — hint visible; create still gated at 12.

**A4-f07 · ORGANIZATION/DOCS (risk) · P3 ·** The app's manifest link is served only by legacy scaffolding the repo
declares non-contract.
- Evidence (read 15:43–15:47): `src/routes/__root.tsx:48` `<link rel="manifest" href="/__grok/manifest.webmanifest">`.
  `find public -iname "*manifest*"` → nothing (15:42): the path is served dynamically by
  `server/middleware/grok-pwa.ts:73` (prod; registered because vite.config.ts sets `serverDir: "./server"`) and
  `scripts/grok-pwa-plugin.mjs:55` (dev). AGENTS.md §Legacy scaffolding calls `server/` middleware + `.grok/`
  "stale scaffolding … **not the contract**", fate undecided (94 §B1 / 96). The same middleware also injects PWA/OG
  head tags.
- Why vs mission: a load-bearing dependency on code marked for possible deletion; if 94 §B1 axes it the head link
  dies silently. README documents no PWA/install capability either way.
- Fix: record the dependency explicitly in 94 §B1/96, or promote the manifest to a static `public/` file (with a
  test), or drop the link + install flow deliberately.
- Verify: `grep -rn "manifest.webmanifest" src/ server/ scripts/` shows one coherent owner; dev + prod both 200.

**A4-f08 · DOCS CONTRADICT EACH OTHER · P2 ·** ".env enables LLM-backed phases" vs "the CLI does not load .env".
- Evidence (read 15:35–15:45): README.md:45 "Environment: `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` in `.env` enables LLM-backed phases
  (P6 synthesis, Chat, expansion)" — directly under the CLI quickstart; scripts/README-cli.md:231-233 "Keys are
  read from `process.env` only (the CLI does **not** load `.env` — export them in the shell, or use `.env`-loading
  tooling around the CLI)."
- Why vs mission: a beginner following the README quickstart puts the key in `.env`, runs the CLI verbs, gets
  keyless skips, and concludes the tool is broken. Two onboarding docs must not disagree on the setup step.
- Fix: split the sentence — "Browser app (`npm run dev`): key in `.env`. CLI: export `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` in the
  shell (the CLI does not read `.env` — README-cli §Live mode)".
- Verify: read both docs; `node scripts/rapidtool-cli.mjs doctor` with `.env` present but unexported → llm not
  configured (matches the corrected wording).

**A4-f09 · JARGON / CONTROL LABEL · P3 ·** "Autopilot · extra gates" — a governance switch that doesn't say what it does.
- Evidence (read 15:40 + 15:48): `console.tsx:2558` bare checkbox `<span>Autopilot · extra gates</span>`, no
  title/caption. Semantics: `store.ts:378-386` `canAutopilot` auto-approves parked NON-stock, NON-forceHuman gates
  whose DoD passes (stock P4/P5/P8/P9 + forceHuman never; E-20 parks + logs `autopilot.gate_denied` on DoD fail).
  The CLI doc states it properly (README-cli.md:70 "auto-approves non-stock, non-forceHuman gates only").
- Why vs mission: the label reads as MORE gates; the effect is fewer human stops under a machine seat. A beginner
  flips governance invisibly; the owner hates jargon.
- Fix: title + caption in the EnforceDodToggle style: "Auto-approve eligible gates (non-stock, DoD-passing).
  Stock P4/P5/P8/P9 and human-only gates still stop."
- Verify: hover text present; park a non-stock gate with autopilot on → ledger row `decidedBy: autopilot`.

**A4-f10 · UI CONSISTENCY (Wild) · P3 ·** Paused-run badge tone reads only the legacy flag.
- Evidence (read 15:47): `console.tsx:1298` badge TEXT uses the canonical `wild.run?.pause === true` → "PAUSED",
  but the warn tint above it (`console.tsx:1288`) is conditioned on the legacy `wild.run?.paused === true`. The
  file's own single-source helper `wildPaused()` (:846) accepts both. A store that sets only the canonical flag
  renders PAUSED in neutral chrome.
- Why vs mission: state color is the operator's fastest read of "run paused at a human gate"; a legacy-flag copy
  of a canonical field is exactly the drift class this wave hunts.
- Fix: use `wildPaused(wild.run)` for both text and tone.
- Verify: construct a stored wildRun with only `pause: true` → badge is warn-toned PAUSED; typecheck/eslint clean.

**A4-f11 · DOCS CURRENCY (numbers) · P2 ·** TA-15's telling is inconsistent across the docs the mission leans on.
- Evidence (read 15:45–15:47): README.md:108-114 "the rewire is fully designed (Wave 4) and lands in **W6** …
  until then, RapidTool does **not** emit a real multi-job product" (corpus census `{stubs: 0, reexports: 5,
  real: 0}`); `remainingwaves.md:12, :27, :233` "TA-15 → W6-L1", "Never claim multi-job emission before TA-15
  lands (`reexports:5, real:0` is the truth)"; vs `taskuniverselog.md:466-468` "**2026-09-17 (wave 8 close) —
  TA-15 ACCEPTANCE MET through the real scaffold path (census `{0,0,3}`)**". Also README.md:139-140 "test counts
  **now** scripts **235/235** + src **1811/1811**" vs its own header's 261/261 + 1957/1957 (README.md:12-14).
- COULD-NOT-TRACE: which telling is current cannot be settled read-only — it needs a fresh keyless emit +
  `doctor --tree` census (A1/A3 territory; I stopped there rather than guess).
- Why vs mission: the M-A maker test acceptance and the "not yet" honesty block both hinge on this number;
  contradictory live docs let either side be quoted.
- Fix: one re-derivation, then update README limit #1 (+ remainingwaves M-A row) to the measured census; change
  the "Progress so far" counts' "now" to "at W5 close".
- Verify: `doctor --tree <fresh emit>` matches the number written into README.

**Journey trace (functional, read-only):** open (`/` HomeScreen → brief ≥12 chars → `create` → `/run/$id`) →
brief (P0 status panel "What's happening" + one next action) → run (`Execute Pn` one step / `Run to next gate`;
`canExecute` gates buttons + `e`/`g`/Ctrl+Enter chords) → WILD (`WildToggle` → `startWildRun`; `WildConsole`
polling feed, Pause/Resume/Stop, honest "stopped after reload" note; `wild.step_timeout` watchdog telemetry) →
gates (HitlDock: evidence bundle, 12+ char rejection + counter, forceHuman override, coded policy refusals) →
emit (`ExportSoftwareBlock` only when complete; says CLI-only + exact `emit --check` command + default dir) →
hand-over (`Export` menu: packet/handoff/copy/download; `Ingest report` panel; phase Notes; packet truncation
banners). All steps trace to real handlers — no dead ends found in the journey; controls map to real store
actions. **Honesty surfaces that held up (do not "fix"):** RunStatusPanel, EnforceDodToggle per-state captions,
ExportSoftwareBlock, SettingsDialog (no client key field), RuntimeLogPane ("Honest silence, not a broken tab"),
ScorecardBlock caveat, PendingFixturesBanner, packet-truncation banners, `lastTest` caption rule, cmd-palette
verb chips ("console only" where no verb exists), ChatPane no-backend/disabled-composer honesty.

### A1 findings — appended by LANE A1 (THE FACTORY CORE). Evidence read 2026-09-26 15:30–15:50 CDT (W24/W25 siblings mid-edit in several of these files — line numbers are as-read today). Full lane log: `fixes/264_lane1_audit_core.md`.

**A1-f01 · engine/wildStep (autonomous loop) · P0 — Wild Mode never calls keyed P6 synthesis; the flagship autonomous loop cannot produce LLM-authored code at all.**
- Evidence (read today): `src/lib/engine.ts:3986` — `wildStep`'s only execution call is `const executed = applyPhaseResult(running, phase);` (the synchronous deterministic floor; it never async-calls the live layer). `upgradeP6Keyed` — the only path that reaches `codegen liveP6Synthesis` — is defined at `src/lib/engine.ts:2869` and has exactly ONE caller, `liveLayer` at `src/lib/engine.ts:2920`. The section comment at `src/lib/engine.ts:2853-2856` claims this seam is "shared by liveLayer AND the wild P6 step — ONE seam for both faces of the product" — that claim is drift: no wild path calls it. Both wild drivers loop `wildStep`: CLI `wild --start` via `headless.ts:3805` (`wildStartRun`) and the console via `store.ts:1105` (Wild Mode loop, 20-step cap). The only keyed-P6 callers that exist: `headless.ts:1887` (`synthesizeCycle` ← `defaultP6Synthesize` ← `driveP6Journey`, i.e. the eval journey) and the console per-phase path (`store.ts:560-580` → `engine.runPhaseAsync` → `liveLayer`).
- Why vs mission: THE MISSION demands a truly autonomous factory driven by a DeepSeek-keyed model. Wild Mode is RapidTool's autonomy face; it structurally cannot call the model. Worse, with `enforceDod` ON (keyed default, `engine.ts:1902-1903`) the P6 floor fails `p6-brief-fit` by construction (`dod.ts:604-632`) and `wildLeavePhase` hard-blocks (`engine.ts:3760-3772`, RT_E_GATE_PENDING) — the run can never complete; with advisory (every console-created project — see A1-f09) it silently "completes" a placeholder-code run. Committed evidence (read today): `research/eval-out/results.json` notes `stall:"P6 DoD unmet — hard gate blocks advance: p6-verified, p6-brief-fit."`, `stopCode:"RT_E_GATE_PENDING"`.
- Proposed fix: route `wildStep`'s P6 edge through the live seam when keyed (`upgradeP6Keyed`/`runPhaseAsync` for phase 6), then the tree-run mint + `recordGateInto` — or delegate the P6 edge to `driveP6Journey`. Minimum honest stopgap: refuse a keyed Wild start loudly instead of presenting a deterministic completion.
- Verification path: `src/lib/wild-step.test.ts` — inject a fake live seam, assert keyed P6 invokes it; funded: `rapidtool wild --start` asserts a `p6.synthesis.attempt` log exists.

**A1-f02 · engine/gates/mint chain · P0 — the "tested" mint is unreachable outside `eval`/`repair`: a chunked (app/game/tool) keyed run can never pass `p6-verified` from run/gate/console/wild.**
- Evidence (read today): chunked trees get `lastTest: null` at synthesis (`codegen.server.ts:3383` in `treeUpgraded`, 3323-3399); the only mint `mintTreeRunTested` (`codegen.server.ts:3450-3489`) is called from exactly two sites, both in the journey/repair drivers: `headless.ts:2100` + `2142` (`driveP6Journey` green branch) and `headless.ts:2681` (`repairTreeDir`). The run paths never tree-run or mint: `headless.ts:350-380` (`runPhase`; live:true only forwards to `liveLayer`), `scripts/rapidtool-cli.mjs:467-536` (`run`/`gate`), `scripts/rapidtool-cli.mjs:2834-2911` (`wild`); the CLI never calls `driveP6Journey` (grep: only a comment at `rapidtool-cli.mjs:1224`). The kindless ≤8-signature single-module path mints via its own verify branch (`codegen.server.ts:2511-2518` `testedLastTestStamp`) — so libraries can converge but apps/games/tools cannot.
- Why vs mission: "games, websites, programs, applications, tools" are exactly the chunked kinds. Today every normal verb (`run --live`, `gate --live`, console phase-run, Wild Mode) hard-stops at P6 with `RT_E_GATE_PENDING` even when synthesis produced a good tree, because `p6-verified` (`dod.ts:583-601`) requires the mint that only the eval path performs.
- Proposed fix: when a live P6 pass lands a `generatedTree` (single-module path already self-mints), run the journey's final step in place — candidate emit → `treeRun` → `applyTreeRunTestedMint` → `recordGateInto` — inside `runPhase`/`gate` (or call `driveP6Journey` from the CLI verbs when keyed).
- Verification path: funded: webapp brief → `gate --live` to the P6 edge → expect P7 reachable (not `RT_E_GATE_PENDING`); headless test injecting a fake synthesis seam asserts the mint is reached from `runUntilGate`.

**A1-f03 · P6 verification integrity · P1 — the "machine-verified (tested)" mint is earned by load+presence, not behaviour: hollow or throwing trees pass every gate.**
- Evidence (read today): `codegen.server.ts:3416-3431` `treeRunTestedEvidence` counts `specs.length>0 && assertions>0` — any spec file that runs with ≥1 passing assertion, regardless of what it asserts. The emitted TREE-mode acceptance is: a `tree.entry` row asserting `Object.keys(treeModule).length > 0` (`scaffold.ts:1412-1421`, passes via the CJS↔ESM `default` interop key even for an empty export surface) and A7 `tree-job` rows that assert "exports are functions" then CALL the first export inside `try { … } catch (e) { if (e instanceof ReferenceError) throw e; }` — every other throw is swallowed by design (`scaffold.ts:1596-1615`). Capability rows assert identifier presence in source text only (`scaffold.ts:1651-1700`). So a tree whose functions all `throw new Error("not implemented")` yields green treeRun → mints `lastTest.label:"tested"` → `p6-verified` PASS (`dod.ts:583-601`); `p6-arch` only checks capability→file identifier mapping + template-stub/TODO(E-15) markers (`dod.ts:650-657`).
- Why vs mission: "GOOD code, not bad code" — the product's strongest verification claim is satisfied without exercising a single demanded capability's behaviour. The tree-run lane's own verdict (`compile-check.server.ts` per-spec TAP counts) is honest about what ran; the MINT over-reads it.
- Proposed fix: (a) make the tree-job row assert the call does not throw + a shape of its return (stop swallowing non-ReferenceError); (b) mint only when ≥1 assertion exercised a demanded capability (tag the generated rows so `treeRunTestedEvidence` can count them); (c) add a `p6-behavior` DoD row fed by that evidence.
- Verification path: RED-first — run `driveP6Journey` fakes with a throwing-stub tree and assert no mint; extend `codegen.server.test.ts`.

**A1-f04 · DoD registry · P1 — no criterion measures behaviour or quality; the phase-6 content gates are identifier-presence proxies (over-credit AND over-block).**
- Evidence (read today): full registry `dod.ts:460-731`; P6's only content probes are `p6-brief-fit` (`dod.ts:610-632` — ≥ ceil(50%) of brief nouns must appear as identifiers in the barrel+tree union) and `p6-arch` (`dod.ts:650-657`). Nothing reads tree-run functional results, coverage, or the emitted UI; P7–P12 gates are artifact-presence only. Flip side: a legitimately-built tree that uses different vocabulary fails `p6-brief-fit` and, keyed+enforceDod, HARD-STOPS the run (`engine.ts:3760-3772`).
- Why vs mission: "GOOD code" as a machine claim needs at least one behavioural criterion; vocabulary presence both over-credits hollow code and over-blocks well-named code. (Behaviour-heavy universality findings belong to A3 — cross-ref.)
- Proposed fix: add behaviour-backed P6 criteria (mint evidence: per-capability executed assertions) and split "floor visibility" (advisory) from "hard block" for the vocabulary proxy so a correct-but-differently-named tree parks for review instead of dying.
- Verification path: build a brief-named hollow tree → `runGate(p,6)` currently PASS; after fix expect FAIL; build a well-behaved tree with off-vocabulary names → expect park-not-die.

**A1-f05 · codegen refusal path · P1 — `partialTreeRefuse` keeps the floor's green `lastTest` while replacing `generatedSource`: stale "self-test passed" evidence over a different artifact.**
- Evidence (read today): `codegen.server.ts:3289-3309` returns `{...project, generatedTree, generatedSource: materialized.entrySource, modelRoute}` and never touches `lastTest`, while `treeUpgraded` explicitly nulls it (`codegen.server.ts:3376-3383`: "A stale green stamp outliving its artifact is exactly how a shipped packet came to record `lastTest.ok:true` while the shipped test exited 1"). Next reader is `p6-test` (`dod.ts:556-563` — `lastTest.ok===true` + non-empty detail).
- Why vs mission: honesty contract (declare-or-strike) — a green gate must describe the artifact on disk. With a partial LLM tree landed, the template self-test verdict survives and `p6-test` reads green for code it never tested (advisory runs then advance on it).
- Proposed fix: null `lastTest` in `partialTreeRefuse` exactly as `treeUpgraded` does.
- Verification path: `codegen.server.test.ts` — seam accepts chunk 0, refuses chunk 1; assert `lastTest === null` on the result.

**A1-f06 · gates / honesty surface · P2 — a hard-DoD stall writes NO gate record, so red-gate censuses read clean while the run is stalled on unmet criteria.**
- Evidence (read today): `recordGateInto` is a no-op unless the phase record is `complete` (`dod.ts:865-870`); `failedGates` counts only complete phases (`dod.ts:851-858`). The hard-DoD block stamps an artifact + paused `wildRun` only (`engine.ts:3702-3714`, `3760-3772`; `headless.ts` `dodBlocked` path). Committed row (read today): `research/eval-out/results.json` — M5 evidence "no red DoD gate records — nothing to hide" sits next to `notes.stall:"P6 DoD unmet — hard gate blocks advance: p6-verified, p6-brief-fit."`
- Why vs mission: an honesty surface that says "nothing to hide" on a run stopped by unmet DoD — the failed criteria exist only in prose notes/logs, not in the machine census a packet reader checks.
- Proposed fix: on a hard block record a distinct `gate.blocked` row `{phase, unmet, evidenceHash, blockedAt}` (new event; phase-record semantics unchanged) and teach censuses/M5 to read it.
- Verification path: `wild-step.test.ts` — after a P6 hard block assert the blocked row exists with the unmet ids.

**A1-f07 · emit/handover · P2 — emit refuses over any stale workspace (RT_E_FOREIGN_FILES) and `--force` KEEPS the foreign files: re-emits either fail or mix trees; the clean fix exists only in run-eval.**
- Evidence (read today): refusal at `headless.ts:1596-1607`; force path `headless.ts:1639-1647` (`survivors: opts.force === true ? foreign : []` — foreign files stay and the following tree-run then judges a mixed tree). The W23-L1 fix landed ONLY in the eval driver: `scripts/run-eval.mjs:783` `prepareCleanEmitDir` called at `:1793`. Committed evidence: `results.json` `notes.emitError:"foreign files in emit dir (pass --force to keep): src/generated/get-property.ts"` + M6=0; the stale tree still sits at `research/eval-out/coffeehouse2home-property-ledger/` (mtime 2026-09-24 14:48).
- Why vs mission: the mission includes "continuously improve / refactor / enhance what it produces" — an iteration loop must re-emit its own workspace; today the CLI/console path refuses or produces mixed-tree evidence the tree-run then weighs.
- Proposed fix: expose a guarded `--clean-target` (eval-driver parity, restricted to a driver-owned dir) on `emit`, or per-file replace actions in the refusal payload.
- Verification path: emit twice into one dir → second emit succeeds (or fails with an explicit clean instruction); eval keeps its clean report.

**A1-f08 · codegen/docs drift · P3 — comments claim P6 synthesis runs on the PRO tier; the code requests and stamps flash.**
- Evidence (read today): `codegen.server.ts:8` "one json:true synthesis call on deepseek-v4-pro"; `:2732` "one pro-tier call PER chunk"; `:2753`, `:3283` "modelRoute deepseek-pro" — but `P6_SYNTH_MODEL = DEEPSEEK_MODELS.flash` (`codegen.server.ts:150`; `= "deepseek-v4-flash"` per `llm/providers/deepseek.ts:53-56`) and the code stamps `modelRoute: "deepseek-flash"` (`codegen.server.ts:3304`, `3385`). Grep: no P6 synthesis call anywhere requests `DEEPSEEK_MODELS.pro`.
- Why vs mission: declare-or-strike; and if flash is a deliberate cost choice, the "GOOD code" bar the docs advertise is being aimed at the cheaper tier — make that an explicit, testable decision (env override + docs) rather than comment drift.
- Proposed fix: correct the comments or make the tier explicit/switchable in one source of truth.
- Verification path: a doc-drift test asserting the comments' tier matches `P6_SYNTH_MODEL`; funded: assert the served model on a synthesis call.

**A1-f09 · engine create / console · P2 — console-created projects are never `enforceDod=true`; the "On by default when a DeepSeek key is configured" caption is false in the app.**
- Evidence (read today): console create runs in the browser — `store.ts:1164-1168` `emptyProject({brief, name})`; `keyedDeepSeekConfigured()` is a `process.env` read that returns false when `process` is undefined (`engine.ts:1820-1826`), so `enforceDod` defaults false (`engine.ts:1902-1903`). The console caption claims the keyed default (`console.tsx:6264-6284`; its own comment cites a stale line ref `engine.ts:1498-1499`).
- Why vs mission: honesty surface overstates enforcement on the primary UI path; combined with A1-f01 a keyed console run advances advisory past failing gates while producing no LLM code.
- Proposed fix: thread the server-reported `llmStatus.configured` into the console create action (or stamp `enforceDod` on first hydrate for keyed projects), or strike the in-app wording.
- Verification path: console test — keyed server status → created project has `enforceDod === true`.

**Trace limits / corrections (A1 lane).** (1) No keyed calls were made (account unfunded — all calls 402 per `fixes/262`), so f01–f03 are code traces; last funded evidence = the 2026-09-24 keyed rows in `research/eval-out/history.json` (best 2026-09-24T19:49:11Z m1=1/m6=1/m7=1 mean 0.667; latest 2026-09-25T00:03:43Z mean 0.333 with `synthesisSucceeded:false`). (3) **`src/lib/gates.ts` does not exist** — gate code lives in `src/lib/dod.ts` + `src/lib/engine.ts` (recorded so siblings don't chase a phantom file). (4) The provider's 402→`RT_E_LLM_NO_BALANCE` classification IS present today (`src/lib/llm/providers/deepseek.ts:431-443`) — the W23-L1 no-balance chain is code-present; its end-to-end proof needs a funded run.

### §A2 — findings (PARENT-COMPLETED from lane A2's own summary; the author hit the call cap before the append — nothing invented, all evidence read by A2 today)
- **A2-f01 · P1 · `fixtures --approve` mints human-approved acceptance fixtures with NO seat/token/provenance.** `rapidtool-cli.mjs:977-998` stamps `acceptanceApprovedAt` + `approval:"approved"` with no actor/`decidedBy`/`RT_HUMAN_SEAT_TOKEN` — while `approve` in the same file (`:566-618`) requires exactly that, and `policy.ts:156-180` deliberately refuses an unverified human seat; README `:22` claims fixtures "stay pending until a human approves". An agent can self-approve the brief-derived bar and the record cannot tell. Fix: route through `evaluatePolicy`/seat+token, or record `decidedVia` and rename the claim. Verify: `fixtures --approve-all` non-TTY + inspect the persisted row.
- **A2-f02 · P1 · `gate.sh` can report GREEN with a red test leg.** `gate.sh:154-178`: `legStats` collects only `not ok N - name` lines; the exit (`:178`) gates on typecheck/lint/assertionGaps/**build** only — `testsScripts`/`testsLib` exits are recorded but never gate `code` → a leg exiting non-zero WITHOUT a TAP line (crash, runner-refusal, missing file) = `ok:true`, exit 0. Fix: add `!gates.testsScripts.ok || !gates.testsLib.ok` to the exit condition.
- **A2-f03 · P2 · Internal errors exit 1, contradicting the documented taxonomy (exit 4) in ≥7 verbs.** `deepen :3022`, `harvest :1912`, `swarm :1937`, `bench :1987`, `design :1833`, `diagram :1868` respond `RT_E_INTERNAL` on exit **1**; `vault :1650-1654` states it. README `:845-846`/`:897` say internal = exit 4 → an agent branching on `exit_code` takes the usage-error path. Fix: exit 4 (or re-code to a 1-class code).
- **A2-f04 · P2 · `eval`'s "no writes" claim is false as written.** `rapidtool-cli.mjs:1018-1024` + USAGE `:3177` claim strictest read-only; `respond()` (`:143-163`) unconditionally appends an NDJSON row via `resolveLogDir(CLI_PROJECT)` → `cwd/.rapidtool/logs`. Fix: narrow the claim to "never writes the eval-out documents".
- **A2-f05 · P2 · Stale `preflightClass:"balance-exhausted"` has no producer.** The live `run-in-progress.json` carries it; `classifyLlmPreflight` (`run-eval.mjs:1017-1165`) emits only `no-balance`/`auth-rejected`/`wire-ok`/…; `grep` → 0 hits. The `eval` verb echoes it verbatim (`scorecard.ts:682`); `preflightLimits()` has no case. Fix: reconcile vocabulary + legacy alias, or render as unknown-class.
- **A2-f06 · P2 · The 3/9 wallet artefact is surfaced too weakly.** Marker: `meanOfNine 2.9997`, `decision retrace-first`, `preflightClass balance-exhausted`; `eval` prints `derived.meanTotal 0.3333` as the headline with only buried evidence; no envelope-level NOT-A-MEASUREMENT flag. Fix: derive a top-level `measurement:false` from the marker's fundedness/no-balance evidence.
- **A2-f07 · P2 · `RAPIDTOOL_TOKEN_CAP` is not a session cap on the codegen path.** `synthWithBackoff` (`codegen.server.ts:2310-2317`) defaults `ledger=emptyLedger()`; both production call sites (`:2817`, `:3174`) pass no ledger → each call starts from ZERO → only a single call exceeding the whole cap can trip. Also `headless.ts:3027` (verify) passes no `budget` → T-107.6 seams inert on the CLI path. Fix: thread one process-scoped ledger (or rename to per-call).
- **A2-f08 · P2 · README-cli.md is stale on `emit --verify-kind` in four places.** The arm exists (`rapidtool-cli.mjs:879-891`, `RT_E_VERIFY_KIND_FAILED`) while README `:30-35`, `:186-190`, `:360-368`, `:431-436`, `:466-470` insist "NOT on disk"; its own reasoning self-refutes (the flag never needed a case arm). Also internal count drift 35 vs 37 (`:363` vs `:31`).
- **A2-f09 · P2 · Two shipped verbs have NO contract section.** `fixtures` + `eval` appear only in the verb list (`:14`) — no `## Fixtures`/`## Eval` sections though README calls itself "the agent-facing contract". Also `:19` lists the lesson family without `--effect` (USAGE `:3199` documents it).
- **A2-f10 · P2/P3 · `fixtures --draft` silently clears `acceptanceApprovedAt`.** `rapidtool-cli.mjs:965` deletes it while `mergeDraftedFixtures` keeps approved rows → approved fixtures with a null approval timestamp read "no".

### §A3 — findings (PARENT-COMPLETED from lane A3's own summary; the author hit the call cap before the append — nothing invented, all evidence read/run by A3 today)
- **A3-f01 · P0 · emitted code cannot load — the fix exists only at synthesis time, not at emit time.** `research/eval-out/coffeehouse2home-property-ledger/src/generated/get-property.ts:250-258` ends in CJS `module.exports.*` while `package.json:5` declares `"type":"module"`; live: `node --experimental-strip-types --test tests/oracle.spec.ts` → `ReferenceError: module is not defined in ES module scope` (`pass 0/fail 1`). Fixed at synthesis (`materializeImportableTree`, `codegen.server.ts:1924,3135`) but NOT at emit: re-emitting the stored project with today's code still writes the CJS file because `scaffold.ts:2543-2565` passes `generatedTree[].source` through **verbatim**. Fix: emit-time materialization/validation (an import-smoke or reuse `materializeImportableTree`) before writing.
- **A3-f02 · P1 · the emit prompt teaches shapes that fail the emitted tsconfig.** `codegen.server.ts:465-492` mandates "TypeScript-style **CommonJS**" + "Assign module.exports"; `scaffold.ts:618` emits `"strict": true`. On the emitted tree: `tsc --strict` → **15× TS7006** + **TS2306 "not a module"** for the 3 bridges → `npm run typecheck` (a README-advertised command) is RED on the shipped artifact.
- **A3-f03 · P1 · partial synthesis = total failure.** Today's emit repro: `43 tests · 9 pass · 28 fail · 6 skipped`; the 28 reds are `capability.9–36` (chunks that were refused). `treeRun ok:false` → default `redTree:"refuse"` blocks emit entirely; the real run shipped only under `redTree:"banner"` with **0 delivery**.
- **A3-f04 · P1 · `emit` has no self-cleaning; the eval driver does.** `run-eval.mjs:783,1793` deletes the prior workspace (`prepareCleanEmitDir`, W23); the CLI emit (`rapidtool-cli.mjs:352,851`) does not → a real agent's second emit hits `RT_E_FOREIGN_FILES` — exactly run #1's failure (`results.json:41-43,124`). Fix: same pre-clean (or the `--force` semantics) on the CLI emit path.
- **A3-f05 · P1 · the graders don't grade the product.** All 7 graders demand `expectedFiles: [package.json, index.html, README.md]` (scaffold infra) and the single `expectedContent` is `package.json contains "test"`. The acceptance layer passes a tree whose generated code is CJS-broken — the bar is unrelated to "GOOD code"/"perfect UI/UX".
- **A3-f06 · P1 · universality is untested.** All 7 fixtures are `kind:"webapp"`; zero game/cli/library/service. The 5-kind taxonomy exists (`scaffold.ts:84,115-121`); only `game` has real depth (canvas shell `:914-935` + `assets/manifest.json:1747`); cli-tool/library/service emit only a throwing stub (`:936-955`). "Any kind of software → games, websites, programs, applications, tools" is **UNMEASURED**.
- **A3-f07 · P2 · emitted UI is a name-dump, and by design can never improve.** `src/ui/console.tsx:14-21` renders `generated exports (N): names`; `scaffold.ts:2567-2570` states the webapp/game UI is deliberately **never** machine-generated → "perfect UI/UX for said software" is structurally out of reach of today's emitter.
- **A3-f08 · P2 · README table overclaims shipping.** `README.md:40-44` lists `src/jobs/track|record|flip|show|lease.ts` as shipped while **no `src/jobs/` exists on disk** (the honest-boundaries line `:93` disagrees with the table).
- **A3-f09 · P3 · game kind declares scripts without support files.** `scaffold.ts:560-566` gives game `build:"vite build"` but `:2529` pushes `vite.config.ts` only for webapp.
- **A3-f10 · P3 · COULD-NOT-TRACE** — whether a fully accepted keyed tree yields a green `typecheck` (no keyed calls permitted).

### §R2 — STATE · PERSISTENCE · GATES · PACKET (LANE R2 · W27 mass investigation). Read window 2026-09-26 15:55–16:15 CDT, HEAD `e3d3fe4`; `packet.ts` md5 `73e52444…`, `store.ts` `29bd412a…`, `io.ts` `e3efdfb2…`, `dod.ts` `1b97f4eb…` (all four read IN FULL today, plus the engine persistence layer they call: `hydrateProject` 2107–2539, `attachAlgorithms` 1193–1222, `stampPacket` 373–375). No product edits, no keyed calls, no full-gate runs. Findings re-verify and WIDEN C1-f01/f02/f04/f05/f06/f10 (C1's IDs cited, never renumbered); full lane log: `fixes/267_lane2_audit_state.md`.

**R2-f01 · P1 · C1-f01 CONFIRMED + WIDENED — the unguarded decision-date render is a live whole-store-wipe vector, and the sibling "clamp" is upper-bound-only (the lower bound throws too).**
- Evidence (read today): `packet.ts:484` — `const when = new Date(d.decidedAt).toISOString();` inside `ledgerRows` — the only unguarded date render, while the SAME file clamps the identical hazard at `:213-214` (`new Date(Math.min(latest, 8.64e15)).toISOString()` — upper bound only; `-1e300` passes `Number.isFinite` AND `Math.min`, then throws). Command proof (ran today): `node -e "new Date(1e300).toISOString()"` → `RangeError: Invalid time value`; same for `new Date(Infinity).toISOString()`.
- The row guard lets it through: `engine.ts:2021-2037 wellFormedDecisionRow` requires only `typeof d.decidedAt === "number"` — `NaN`/`±Infinity`/`±1e300` all pass; `engine.ts:2203` keeps those rows; the render then throws.
- The wipe chain (each hop read today): persist reload → `store.ts:839-858 onRehydrateStorage` → `store.ts:1733-1744 seedIfEmpty` (`.filter(isRepairableProjectEntry).map((p) => hydrateProject(p))` — NO per-project try/catch) → `engine.ts:2538 return attachAlgorithms(filled)` (fires whenever ANY repair / hw.stopped / missing packet-lattice-specGraph) → `engine.ts:1193-1222 attachAlgorithms` → `next.packet = compileAgentPacket(next)` (`:1220`, no try/catch) → `packet.ts:484` throws → the throw exits `seedIfEmpty` → `store.ts:850-857` catch → `store.ts:812-815 recoverFromCorruptPersistence` → `set({ projects: [], activeId: null, demoSeeded: false })` + demo seed; the persist middleware then WRITES the emptied state over the blob → every user project is gone. The same throw also escapes `execute`/`approve`/`refreshPacket` (`store.ts:1180-1199`, `:1507-1565`, `:1699-1707` — no try/catch) as a UI crash.
- Why vs mission: honesty contract + "the CODE ITSELF must be professional" — one JSON-representable hostile field (`decidedAt: 1e300` survives `JSON.parse`) destroys the operator's whole workspace, and the failure mode is a silent re-seed.
- **Fix spec (2 layers + pins):** (1) `packet.ts`: add a module-local total helper — `function isoFromMs(ms: number): string { const n = typeof ms === "number" && Number.isFinite(ms) ? ms : 0; return new Date(Math.min(Math.max(n, -8.64e15), 8.64e15)).toISOString(); }` — use it at `:484` (`const when = isoFromMs(d.decidedAt);`) and at `:214` (replace the bare `new Date(Math.min(...))` with `isoFromMs(latest)`), fixing BOTH the missing lower bound and the duplication in one move. Non-finite → epoch (deterministic, parseable); finite-but-out-of-range → clamped (never throws). (2) `engine.ts:2034`: tighten the row guard — `typeof d.decidedAt === "number" && Number.isFinite(d.decidedAt)` (NaN/±Infinity are not real ledger stamps). Do NOT drop finite out-of-range rows (`1e300` is JSON-carryable and the ledger is append-only evidence — the render now clamps it). (3) Pins, RED first: (a) `packet.test.ts` — compile a project whose decision carries `decidedAt: 1e300` (and one with `NaN`) → must NOT throw, must render a clamped ISO; plus a `createdAt: -1e300` case for `compiled_at`; (b) `store.test.ts` — the wipe regression: persist a healthy project PLUS one with `decidedAt: 1e300` + a repair trigger (e.g. one junk log row), rehydrate → the healthy project SURVIVES (today it is replaced by the demo seed); (c) an engine/hydrate case: `decidedAt: Infinity` row → dropped with `decisions:-1` in the `hydrate.repaired` detail.
- Done-when: the three pins pass; `grep -n "new Date(" src/lib/packet.ts` returns only inside `isoFromMs`; the wipe regression shows both projects present post-reload.

**R2-f02 · P1 · C1-f02 CONFIRMED + WIDENED — the "174-1" wild-run guard was applied in ONE async action; `runUntilHitlAsync` plus five siblings silently roll back a wild run started/stopped/paused mid-flight.**
- Evidence (read today): the guard exists only at `store.ts:1290-1294` (`executeAsync`): `// 174-1: do not clobber an in-flight wild run with a stale execute snapshot.` + `if (live.wildRun?.active === true) return { ...committed, wildRun: live.wildRun };`. `runUntilHitlAsync`'s commit (`store.ts:1345-1351`) calls only `rebaseUserFields(current, live)` — and `rebaseUserFields` (`:247-267`) deliberately does NOT copy `wildRun` (comments `:243-245`, `:256-258`: engine-owned run state; the wild driver's own commits must win). So any async commit computed from a pre-wild snapshot reverts `wildRun` (a just-started active run → null/stale; `stopWildRun`/`toggleWildPause` effects lost). Same missing guard at: `deepenResearch` `:874-876`; `runVerifyAsync` `:905-907`; `runP6Journey` `:952-956`; `runModuleReal` `:1399-1405`; `rejectAsync` `:1615-1621`. Only `commitWild` (`:1084-1095`) may own `wildRun` (it merges the driver's own generation).
- Why vs mission: Wild Mode is the autonomy face; a concurrent console action silently resurrecting/killing run state mid-drive breaks the one-driver honest-lifecycle contract and can double-drive or strand a run.
- **Fix spec:** add next to `rebaseUserFields` — `function keepLiveWildRun(committed: Project, live: Project): Project { return live.wildRun?.active === true ? { ...committed, wildRun: live.wildRun } : committed; }` — and apply it at the SIX non-driver commit sites (`executeAsync` refactored onto it, same condition — no behavior change there). Do NOT touch `commitWild`. Keep the minimal condition (`active === true`) for this fix; a phase-2 generalization ("live wildRun always wins for non-driver commits") is a separate scoped change — do not smuggle it in.
- Pins (RED first): in `store.test.ts` — with a paused-active run persisted (the PA-01 shape at `:1038`), dispatch `runUntilHitlAsync` and let it settle (it currently replaces `wildRun` with the stale snapshot) → after the fix `live.wildRun.active === true` + `pause === true` survive; repeat once for `runModuleReal`. Keep the existing `executeAsync` reference pin.
- Done-when: both new pins green; `grep -n "keepLiveWildRun\|174-1" src/lib/store.ts` shows the helper + six call sites; no commit site calls `rebaseUserFields` without either `keepLiveWildRun` or driver context.

**R2-f03 · P1 · C1-f04 CONFIRMED + WIDENED — io's sanitizer and `hydrateProject` are two divergent validators for the SAME payload: crew/oracle/chatLog row junk is filtered only on the import path (the localStorage path keeps it and the packet render throws — R2-f01's wipe class); the scorecard predicate disagrees in BOTH directions; url-less research items survive io but are dropped on reload.**
- Evidence: engine hydrate keeps rows raw — `engine.ts:2405` `oracle: Array.isArray(raw.oracle) ? raw.oracle : []`, `:2406` same for `crew`, `:2238` same for `chatLog`; io filters rows — `io.ts:213 defendRowArray(raw, "chatLog", …)`, `:217` (oracle, `typeof row.name === "string"`), `:221` (crew), pinned by `io.test.ts:490-593` (its own words: "a null row used to crash the packet crew render"). The render that crashes: `packet.ts:526-528` (`c.role`, `c.active`, `c.budgetTokens`, `c.mandate`, `c.handoff`) and `:535` (`t.name`, `t.given`, `t.expect`) — a `null` row throws inside `compileAgentPacket`, and via R2-f01's chain that is the wipe/crash. Note `rowRepaired` (`engine.ts:2221-2227`) counts only artifacts/logs/decisions/memory — crew/oracle/chatLog junk triggers NO repair note, so the payload "hydrates cleanly".
- Scorecard divergence (both directions): `io.ts:247-262` keeps any plain-object card whose `axes`/`bottlenecks`/`attackVectors` are arrays, filtering axes to plain objects; `engine.ts:2290-2301 scorecardOk` additionally requires a number `overall` and EVERY axis row to carry a string `label`. (a) `{overall:"8", axes:[], bottlenecks:[], attackVectors:[]}` → io keeps silently (no note), engine drops with `scorecard:dropped`; (b) `{overall:8, axes:[null,{label:"a",…}], …}` → the import path KEEPS the card (io filtered the null first, engine's `every()` then passes) while the reload path DROPS it entirely — same payload, different final state depending on the entry path. That is exactly the drift class the mission's "best possible code" bar forbids.
- research divergence: `io.ts:197-199` keeps items that are plain objects (no `url` requirement); `engine.ts:2257-2259` requires `typeof item.url === "string"` → import keeps an item the reload drops (different corpus + an extra repair row).
- **Fix spec:** (1) move the three row filters INTO `hydrateProject` beside the existing row filters (`engine.ts:2197-2210`): chatLog → plain object; oracle → plain object + `name` string; crew → plain object; add them to `rowRepaired` + `rowTokens` (`crew:-N`, `oracle:-N`, `chatLog:-N`) so repairs stay loud; io keeps its filter as belt-and-braces but shares the SAME predicate (or the io duplicate is deleted — the parity pin then proves equivalence). (2) Export `scorecardUsable(v: unknown): v is Project["scorecard"]` from `engine.ts` (body = current `scorecardOk`, `:2290-2298`) and use it as io's keep/drop decision; io's axes row filter uses the same `label: string` row predicate so import and reload agree. (3) Single research-item predicate (plain object + `url` string) shared by both. (4) Pins: engine-side hydrate test mirroring `io.test.ts:490-593` (`crew:[null]`, `oracle:[null,{no name}]`, `chatLog:[null]`) asserting dropped rows + loud tokens; a parity test over scorecard cases (a)+(b) asserting `importProject` and direct `hydrateProject` produce IDENTICAL scorecard + IDENTICAL repair tokens.
- Done-when: parity pins green; `grep -n "crew: Array" src/lib/engine.ts` shows a filtered assignment; compiling a hydrated project with previously-junk rows never throws.

**R2-f04 · P2 · C1-f05 CONFIRMED — four gate criteria hang on the literal em-dash " — " inside a prose reason; the coupling is UNPINNED, so a cosmetic edit of the policy reason builder silently flips legitimate approvals to FAIL (fail-closed → hard-DoD blocks + red `failedGates` censuses).**
- Evidence: `dod.ts:108-116 provenanceConsistent` — `if (typeof via.reason !== "string" || !via.reason.includes(" — ")) return false;` (line 114); the reasons are minted in `policy.ts:235/:265` (`human-seat-token-verified — ${rawAction} on ${rawResource} …`) and `:262` (`autopilot-seat — …`), under the module's documented reason contract (`policy.ts:32-33`: "starts with a kebab id, then " — " and a human clause"). Writer→field: `headless.ts:787+` mints `HitlDecision.decidedVia` (`types.ts:200`), `:901 reason: verdict.reason`; consumer: `dod.ts:131-143 policyConsistentApproval` → `:145-152 approved()` → criteria `p4-approved` `:530-533`, `p5-complete` `:542-545`, `p8-complete` `:682-685`, `p9-complete` `:694-697`. `gates.test.ts` has ZERO provenance/216o coverage (grep: 0 hits for `decidedVia|provenance`) — nothing detects a separator drift.
- Why vs mission: a gate must fail on evidence, not on prose cosmetics; a whitespace-normalizing refactor of one policy line would turn every human/autopilot approval carrying `decidedVia` into "unmet" → with `enforceDod` ON the run hard-blocks, and `failedGates`/`sealedWithFailures` report red on a perfect build.
- **Fix spec:** in `policy.ts` export ONE contract constant `export const POLICY_REASON_SEPARATOR = " — ";` (interpolated into `:235/:262/:265`) — or, stronger, `export function reasonHasIdAndClause(reason: string): boolean` checking `/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]* — \S/` (id + separator + non-empty clause) — and have `dod.ts:114` consume the export instead of the literal. Add the missing pin (`gates.test.ts`, or `headless.test.ts` beside the existing `decidedVia` pins at `:1604-1625`): for both seats the minted `reason` passes the check, and a reason without the separator fails — so writer and checker move together.
- Done-when: the new pin exists and passes; `grep -n '" — "' src/lib/policy.ts src/lib/dod.ts` shows ONE literal (the constant) shared by build + check.

**R2-f05 · P2 · C1-f06 CONFIRMED — `packet.ts`'s render layer assumes a fully hydrated project while its sibling modules treat hand-built/partial shapes as supported input; the loose `lastTest` render even prints a false "pass".**
- Evidence: `dod.ts:866-868` states the posture explicitly ("a minimal or hand-built shape (`{ } as Project`, phases/logs/artifacts absent) must be a clean no-op (same reference), never a TypeError mid-append") and uses `?? []` throughout; `packet.ts` — the file whose output IS the agent contract — reads several fields unguarded: `:465` `a.body.length` (no string check), `:483` `project.decisions.map` + `:531` `e.fields.map` (no array/row check), `:666` `project.scorecard.axes.map(...)` + `bottlenecks.join` + `attackVectors.join`, `:526-528`/`:535` crew/oracle rows (R2-f03). Honesty sub-case: `:605` renders `project.lastTest.ok ? "pass" : "FAIL"` — a hand-edited `{ok: "yes"}` prints "pass" while the machine gate `p6-test` (`dod.ts:556-562`) demands `ok === true`; prose and gate disagree on the same field. Reachability: `compileAgentPacket` is called by `stampPacket` (`engine.ts:373-375` → store `approve/execute/refreshPacket`, no try/catch) and by `emptyProject`'s tail (`engine.ts:1914`); hydrate's shortcut (`engine.ts:2525-2537`) can return a project whose crew/oracle junk was never filtered (R2-f03) — so "assume hydrated" is not actually guaranteed.
- **Fix spec:** add two module-local helpers in `packet.ts` — `const str = (v: unknown): string => (typeof v === "string" ? v : "");` and `const rows = <T>(v: unknown): T[] => (Array.isArray(v) ? (v as T[]) : []);` — then route: `:465` `const body = str(a.body); … (body.length > 900 ? \`${body.slice(0, 900)}…\` : body)`; `:483` `rows<HitlDecision>(project.decisions).map(...)` (skip non-object rows); `:526-528` `rows(project.crew).map((c) => \`- ${str(c.role)}${c.active === true ? " [ACTIVE]" : ""} (${Number.isFinite(c.budgetTokens) ? c.budgetTokens : 0} tok): ${str(c.mandate)} → ${str(c.handoff)}\`)` — keep the exact current string for valid rows; `:531` `rows(e.fields).map(...)`; `:535` `rows(project.oracle).slice(0, 8)`; `:666` `rows(project.scorecard.axes).map(...)`, `rows<string>(bottlenecks).join("; ")`, same for attackVectors; `:605` render `project.lastTest.ok === true ? "pass" : "FAIL"` (declare-or-strike: a non-boolean ok is not a pass). Choose every default so hydrated projects render byte-identically.
- Pins: `packet.test.ts` — a `{ } as Project`-minimal compile does not throw; a project with `artifacts:[{body missing}], contracts:{entities:[{name:"x"}]}, scorecard:{axes:[null]}` compiles (renders fallback lines); a `lastTest:{ok:"yes",detail:"x"}` renders "FAIL". Done-when: new pins green + a self-audit grep shows no unguarded `.map(`/`.length` over project collections in `packet.ts`.

**R2-f06 · P2 · C1-f10 CONFIRMED — the four duplicated transition/stop-reason implementations, plus a 3rd hard-DoD copy inside `finishPhase`, plus a 5th copy the brief did not name (`store.recordGate` vs `dod.recordGateInto` — the documented delegation never happened).**
- Evidence (read today, compared side-by-side): `advanceOnce` (`store.ts:415-478`) vs `advanceOnceAsync` (`:604-661`) — guard ladder verbatim-identical per branch (blocked+rejected `:422-424`/`:612-614`; locked `:425`/`:615`; awaiting_hitl+autopilot `:426-450`/`:616-635`; complete<12 + hard-DoD `:451-465`/`:636-648`; illegal transition `:467`/`:650`; prior-seal `:468`/`:651`), differing only in `runReady` vs `runReadyAsync`. `runUntilHitl` (`:1201-1259`) vs `runUntilHitlAsync` (`:1300-1356`) — identical loop ceiling (`(13 - currentPhase) * 2 + 2`), identical stop-reason ladder (`dod→hitl→blocked→complete→error`, `:1220-1228` vs `:1318-1326`), identical result shaping (`:1256-1258` vs `:1352-1355`). `runReady` (`:480-498`) vs `runReadyAsync` (`:582-601`) duplicate the `running`-stamp. The hard-DoD block literal appears THREE times (`:455-464`, `:638-647`, `:520-529`). And `store.recordGate` (`:316-357`) is a near-verbatim twin of `dod.recordGateInto` (`dod.ts:865-909`) — whose header says "so the store can delegate to this function"; the store still imports only `gateArtifact/hardDodBlockInto/hardDodMessage/runGate` (`store.ts:21`) and keeps its own copy (the dod one is the more defensive: `?? []` reads). C1's comparison found no live divergence — correct as of these bytes; the risk is that every future fix must land 2–5×, and drift is invisible.
- Why vs mission: "the CODE ITSELF must be professional quality — THE BEST POSSIBLE" — five copies of the state machine is the largest drift surface in the store; the refactor is mechanical and behavior-preserving and is the cheapest point to add the parity pins this wave wants.
- **Fix spec (three mechanical steps; each ships alone; no behavior change permitted):** (1) `decideTransition(project, autopilot): Transition` — a pure module-local helper returning a tagged union (`{kind:"error"|"park"|"deny"|"approve-autopilot"|"dod-block"|"advance"|"run-ready", …}`) capturing the whole `:419-477` ladder; `advanceOnce`/`advanceOnceAsync` become thin executors switching on the tag (keep every error string byte-identical — do NOT normalize prose). (2) `stopReasonFor(project, dodBlocked): RunStopReason` — the pure ladder used by both loops, plus `runIterationCeiling(currentPhase)` for the shared `maxRuns` formula. (3) `runUntilCoreAsync(start, advance, autopilot, commit)` — the shared async loop; `runUntilHitlAsync` delegates. The sync `runUntilHitl` CANNOT delegate (sync return type; `store.test.ts` pins it) — it keeps its own loop but consumes `stopReasonFor` + `runIterationCeiling` so the two ladders can never drift. (4) `store.recordGate` → `return recordGateInto(project, phase);` (import from dod.ts; delete the body). Identical for hydrated projects (the only reachable shape) and strictly more defensive for hand-built ones.
- Pins that must be green BEFORE and AFTER (existing): `store.test.ts:478/502/515/533/551/568/576` (runUntilHitl outcome rows), `:1057` (enforceDod block artifact+log+cursor), `:1613` (executeAsync hard-DoD ok:false), `:716-740` (ST-6 serialization/rollback), `:310/:324/:366` (autopilot policy), `:256` (gate verdicts recorded on completion AND approval), `gates.test.ts` recordGateInto suite. New pins: (a) a `decideTransition` matrix over the 9 states asserting tag + error text; (b) a sync/async parity test — one seeded project through `runUntilHitl` and `runUntilHitlAsync` (deterministic synth) yields identical `{ran, stoppedReason}` and the same committed cursor; (c) a recordGate delegation pin comparing `store.recordGate(p, ph)` output shape with `recordGateInto(p, ph)` on the same fixture.
- Done-when: ladder sites 4→2 (guard core + stop ladder); `grep -c "Waiting on human approval" src/lib/store.ts` = 1; parity pins green.

**R2-f07 · P2 · OV-13 gap on the STORE face — the console's live-degrade paths carry no coded row: a keyed engine failure reads like a keyless run, a configured-LLM guidance failure leaves no trace, and both async run actions can reject instead of returning their documented envelope.**
- Evidence: `store.ts:560-580 synthesizeAsync` — `} catch { // engine.runPhaseAsync unavailable or threw → deterministic fallback below. }` — the bare catch is the ONLY handling of a throwing live layer on the console path: no log row, no `RT_E_*`, the phase silently becomes deterministic; the engine's own doctrine (OV-13, `engine.ts:2932`, `:3033-3065`, `:3289-3305`: "a keyed failure must never read like a keyless run … a CODED row for a live-layer degrade that used to be comment-only") is not honored on this face. `store.ts:1624-1626` — `catch { /* guidance optional — never block the rejection */ }` — a configured LLM's `interpretFeedback` failure is invisible. `executeAsync` (`:1264-1299`) and `runUntilHitlAsync` (`:1300-1356`) wrap nothing: any throw inside rejects the returned promise (`serialized()` `:196-210` contains only the CHAIN tail, not the caller's promise) while the store's contract and every sibling action (`startWildRun` codes every refusal, `:988-1046`) promise a result envelope. Run-result `code` fields: `startWildRun` sets `RT_E_*` on refusals; the run/envelope family does not (`RunResult`/`ApproveResult` shapes at `:37-46`).
- **Fix spec:** (1) `synthesizeAsync`: `catch (err)` → build row `{ts: Date.now(), phase, level: "warn", event: \`p${phase}.live.degrade\`, detail: \`${code} — live synthesis failed: ${clipped message} — deterministic floor used instead\`}` where `code = classifyError(err)` mapped through the errors.ts registry (never an unregistered string — mirror `engine.ts liveDegradeCode` `:3314+`), append to `project.logs` capped 200, and return that project from the deterministic fallback (`applyPhaseResult(projectWithRow, phase)`). (2) `rejectAsync`: on failure while `llmStatus.configured`, append an `llm.guidance.failed` warn row with the coded reason; skip when keyless. (3) `executeAsync`/`runUntilHitlAsync`: wrap the bodies so any throw returns `{ ok: false, ran, stoppedReason: null, error: String(err), code: "RT_E_INTERNAL" }` (mirror `startWildRun`'s catch `:1127-1136`) and commits nothing. Optionally extend `RunResult`'s error arm with `code?: ErrorCode` — only with the pin below updated.
- Pins: `store.test.ts` — (a) run a phase with a hostile decision stamp (`decidedAt: 1e300`) through `executeAsync` → after this fix the promise RESOLVES `{ok:false, code:"RT_E_INTERNAL"}` (today it rejects); (b) inject a failing `runPhaseAsync` seam (the module's DI idiom — dynamic-import override like `runP6Journey` `:923-968`, or a red-first unit on `synthesizeAsync` once exported for tests) asserting the `p<phase>.live.degrade` row + the deterministic result; (c) configured-status `reject` with a throwing `interpretFeedback` → `llm.guidance.failed` row present. Done-when: the three pins green; `grep -n "catch {" src/lib/store.ts` shows bare catches only in the documented persistence/telemetry set (`:790`, `:803`, `:853`, `:1482`, `:1499`, `:1649`).

### §R1 — THE PHASE ENGINE CORE (engine.ts, phases.ts) — appended by W27 lane R1 · reads 2026-09-26 16:00–16:15 CDT · HEAD `e3d3fe4` · lane log `fixes/267_lane1_audit_engine.md`

**R1-f01 · P0 · `wildStep` never calls the keyed P6 seam — A1-f01 RE-VERIFIED on today's bytes, with the EXACT fix.**
- Evidence (read 16:02–16:07): `src/lib/engine.ts:3986` — `wildStep`'s execute branch's ONLY execution call is `const executed = applyPhaseResult(running, phase);`; nothing in `wildStep` (3820-4033) is async-keyed. `upgradeP6Keyed` (engine.ts:2869) has exactly ONE caller repo-wide: `engine.ts:2920` (`liveLayer`); `grep -rn upgradeP6Keyed src/ scripts/` → def :2869 · comment :2917 · call :2920. The seam comment `engine.ts:2852-2856` still claims "shared by liveLayer AND the wild P6 step — ONE seam for both faces of the product" — drift. The recorded design `fixes/154_direction.md:233-241` (D7 step 2) and `fixes/149_a5_synthesis_fixes.md:195` say the same fix was owed and never landed. Both wild drivers loop `wildStep`: `headless.ts:3805` (`wildStartRun`), `headless.ts:3624` (`wildStartHeadless`); CLI arm `scripts/rapidtool-cli.mjs:2834-2911`; console loop `store.ts:1105`.
- Why vs mission: the autonomy face of a "truly autonomous software factory" cannot call the model on the one phase that writes code. With `enforceDod` ON (keyed default) the P6 floor fails `p6-brief-fit` and `wildLeavePhase` hard-blocks (`engine.ts:3760-3772`, RT_E_GATE_PENDING); advisory runs "complete" on placeholder code.
- Fix (coder-ready, engine.ts only):
  1. `WildStepOptions` (engine.ts:3515-3518) gains additive `p6Edge?: (p: Project) => Promise<Project>` (test seam; absent = production).
  2. New module-private helper beside `wildStep`: `async function wildP6Edge(project)` → dynamic-import the concatenated specifier `"./head" + "less.ts"` (the `engine.ts:3908` idiom — client-bundle-safe), call `hl.runP6Edge(project)` (the ONE P6-edge seam added in R1-f02), fallback `applyPhaseResult(project, 6)` on any throw/missing export.
  3. Call site at engine.ts:3986 becomes: `const executed = phase === 6 && (opts?.p6Edge ?? (keyedDeepSeekConfigured() ? wildP6Edge : null)) ? await (opts?.p6Edge ?? wildP6Edge)(running) : applyPhaseResult(running, phase);` — guard = `phase === 6` + keyed, via the engine's own bare-Node-safe probe `keyedDeepSeekConfigured()` (:1820-1826; parity with `isDeepSeekConfigured` = `Boolean(env("DEEPSEEK_API_KEY"))`, `llm/providers/deepseek.ts:122-128`). Keyless ⇒ exact current bytes.
  4. Log honesty: `wild.exec` detail must name the edge used ("P6 executed (keyed synthesis edge)" vs the current "P${phase} executed (deterministic floor)") — fixes/154 D7's rule ("must not log deterministic floor while a key is set").
  - Do NOT stop at `upgradeP6Keyed` alone: for chunked kinds (app/tool/game) synthesis alone still cannot pass `p6-verified` (the minted `lastTest` comes only from a tree-run — R1-f02); the wild edge must reach the same close-the-edge seam as the CLI run.
- Pin (keyless-runnable now): extend `src/lib/wild-step.test.ts` (fixture :14-40, normalizer :184-197 exists): (a) phase-6-ready fixture + injected `p6Edge` fake → assert called exactly once, outcome/logs prove the seam ran; (b) same fixture, no `p6Edge`, no `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` → assert byte-parity with the deterministic floor path. Funded done-when (coder follow-up): `rapidtool wild --start` → `p6.journey.converged` (or `p6.tree.tested.skipped`) log + P7 reachable, no `RT_E_GATE_PENDING`.

**R1-f02 · P0 · the "tested" mint is unreachable from `run --live` / `gate --live` / `wild` — A1-f02 RE-VERIFIED + the one-seam fix (`runP6Edge`).**
- Evidence (read 16:05–16:12): `headless.ts:350-380` `runPhase` — live:true only forwards to `liveLayer` (line 359); no tree-run/mint anywhere in it. `headless.ts:462` `runReady` → `runPhase(...,{live})`; `headless.ts:466-509` `finishPhase` records the gate only. CLI `run`/`gate` → `runUntilGate` (`rapidtool-cli.mjs:467-470`; `maxSteps 1` for `run`). Mint callers repo-wide today: `headless.ts:2100`/`:2142` (driveP6Journey green branch) and `:2681`/`:2687`/`:2699` (repairTreeDir) ONLY; chunked trees get `lastTest:null` at synthesis (`codegen.server.ts:3383` in `treeUpgraded`, read today), and the only re-mint is `mintTreeRunTested` (`codegen.server.ts:3450`, self-guarded). So `run --live`/`gate --live` can synthesize a tree but can never reach `p6-verified` → hard DoD stop (matches the committed `research/eval-out/results.json` stall evidence).
- Fix (coder-ready, headless.ts + one engine call): add ONE exported seam and use it everywhere:
  1. `headless.ts` new export `runP6Edge(project: Project, deps?: Partial<P6JourneyDeps>): Promise<Project>`: `const j = await driveP6Journey(project, deps ? { deps } : {}); return j.outcome === "keyless" ? applyPhaseResult(project, 6) : j.project;` (keyless = byte-identical to today's `liveLayer(p,6)` — the floor already re-stamps the reqTrace at `engine.ts:1527`).
  2. `runPhase` signature gains additive `p6Deps?: Partial<P6JourneyDeps>`; in the live branch, `if (phase === 6) return await runP6Edge(project, opts.p6Deps);` placed before the generic `liveLayer` call (headless.ts:358).
  3. `wildStep` reaches it via R1-f01's `wildP6Edge` — ONE seam for run / gate / wild; the console `executeAsync` path (`store.ts:560-580` → `engine.runPhaseAsync` → `liveLayer`) should ALSO call it (engine-side: `runPhaseAsync` phase-6 branch → dynamic-import headless `runP6Edge` when keyed; browser probe false ⇒ floor, unchanged).
  - Caveat for the coder: `driveP6Journey` returns `"failed"` (with the P6 record possibly green + `lastTest` minted) when acceptance fixtures are pending (headless.ts:2115-2141). For fixture-less wild/run there are no fixtures; if a caller wants "failed ⇒ park", branch on the result — decide explicitly in the landing log.
- Pin: `headless.test.ts` (driveP6Journey fakes at :403-880 are the template): new test — P6-edge project + fake `p6Deps` (synthesize returns tree-carrying project; runTree green) → `runPhase(p, 6, {live:true, p6Deps})` returns a project with `lastTest.label === "tested"` and a recorded passing P6 gate. Funded done-when: `gate --live` on a webapp brief reaches P7 (no RT_E_GATE_PENDING); `run --live` at the P6 edge returns `p6-verified` passed.

**R1-f03 · P2 · modelRoute honesty leak — C1-f03 RE-VERIFIED (the pro call is stamped "deepseek-flash").**
- Evidence (read 16:08–16:12): `src/lib/llm-tasks.ts:127` (`extractConstraints`), `:157` (`interpretFeedback`), `:188` (`phaseNarrative`) all request `model: "deepseek-v4-pro"`. The provider serves pro for that id: `llm/providers/deepseek.ts:343-347` (`model = opts.model ?? flash`; `isPro = model === DEEPSEEK_MODELS.pro`) + `:388-394` (pro gets thinking-enabled shape). `phases.ts:89` documents "deepseek-v4-pro, served as `deepseek-v4-pro`". But `engine.ts:2960-2968` stamps `modelRoute: "deepseek-flash"` whenever `llmRan && base.modelRoute !== "deepseek-pro"` → after any successful non-research live phase (`liveLayer` :3021 `phaseNarrative` / :3044 `extractConstraints`) the packet (`packet.ts:121 model_route`) and console (`console.tsx:2540`) name flash while pro served. `emptyProject`'s own comment (`engine.ts:1869-1874`) claims liveLayer stamps "deepseek-flash / deepseek-pro" — pro is unreachable by construction.
- Fix: thread the served model, don't guess: (a) `LlmResult` (`llm.ts`) gains `model?: LlmModel` set from the request id (provider already knows it at `deepseek.ts:343`); (b) `NarrativeResult`/`ConstraintsResult`/`FeedbackResult` (`llm-tasks.ts`) carry `model`; (c) `liveLayer` replaces `llmRan: boolean` with `servedRoute: "deepseek-flash" | "deepseek-pro" | null`, set per successful call (fetchResearch's narrative call uses no model ⇒ flash; llm-tasks results ⇒ their reported model); the stamp uses `servedRoute` with the existing "never overwrite a concrete route" rule reduced to "set when base is deterministic/none" (first stamp wins, value TRUE). (d) Update the `emptyProject` comment + `engine.ts:2834-2836` E-14 comment to match.
- Pin: unit — `routeForLlmModel()` pure mapper test + `createLlmTasks` fake-completer test asserting `NarrativeResult.model === "deepseek-v4-pro"` (registry.test.ts:590 fake pattern). Funded done-when: `run --live` P2 → packet `model_route: deepseek-pro` (or flash for P0/P7/P11 narratives — whichever served).

**R1-f05 · P1 · `store.runP6Journey` is an UNWIRED action — zero callers; the console's only mint-reaching control does not exist. [NEW]**
- Evidence (read 16:13): declaration `store.ts:130-147`, implementation `store.ts:923-957`; `grep -rn "runP6Journey" src/ scripts/` → ONLY those two lines (self-references); `grep -n "runP6Journey" src/components/console.tsx` → 0 hits. So the store action that lazily imports `headless.driveP6Journey` (the mint path) is dead code from the UI's perspective; the console's per-phase live path (`executeAsync` → `runPhaseAsync`) cannot mint (R1-f02), and Wild cannot either (R1-f01).

Additionally the action's own comment (`store.ts:928-934`) says "in the browser the import itself fails … the catch below returns the honest refusal" — so even after wiring, a browser-rendered control needs a server-fn/host bridge (design decision for R6/R2, not solved here).

Store side is ready; no store change required beyond ensuring the returned `{ok:false,error}` renders.

**R1-f06 · P3 · dead disjunct `p.kind === null` — C1-f11 RE-VERIFIED.**
- Evidence (read 16:03): `engine.ts:2883` `if (p.kind === undefined || p.kind === null)`. Producers cannot emit `null`: `emptyProject` spreads `kind` only when truthy (`:1849`), `hydrateProject` deletes every non-legal value including null (`:2425-2427`), and `types.ts:323` types `kind?: "app" | "tool" | "game"` (no null). Only a hand-forged JS object reaches it.

**R1-f07 · P2 · decision-row guard accepts non-finite `decidedAt` (whole-store-wipe class) — C1-f01 engine half.**
- Evidence (read 16:03): `engine.ts:2021-2037` `wellFormedDecisionRow` checks `typeof d.decidedAt === "number"` only → `1e300`/`Infinity` survive hydrate; the same file documents the hazard class at `:2242-2249` ("ONE corrupt row wiped the whole store on reload") and `packet.ts:193-215` clamps the identical `toISOString` hazard while `packet.ts:484` reads the raw value. Owner split: R2 owns the packet clamp; THIS row is the engine-side guard.
- Fix: `typeof d.decidedAt === "number" && Number.isFinite(d.decidedAt) && Math.abs(d.decidedAt) <= 8.64e15` (the pack` toISOString` bound). Dropped rows already log `decisions:-N` via `hydrate.repaired`.
- Done-when: import fixture with `decidedAt: 1e300` → hydrate drops the row loudly, `compileAgentPacket` does not throw.

**R1-f08 · P3 · engine/io scorecard validators diverge — C1-f04 engine half (single-validator fix).**
- Evidence (read 16:03): `engine.ts:2290-2301` `scorecardOk` requires object + numeric `overall` + every axis a string-labelled object + array `bottlenecks`/`attackVectors`; `io.ts:247-262` keeps any object axes rows. One payload can read "kept" in io and get dropped by the engine. 
- Fix: extract `usableScorecard(value): boolean` from the engine criterion and import it in `io.ts`'s hydrate (R2's file) — ONE validator; io's "kept" message then matches engine behavior. No behavior change needed on the engine side beyond the export.

**R1-f09 · P3 · `upgradeP6Keyed`'s catch is silent (keyed throw ⇒ no coded row). [NEW, OV-13 sibling]**
- Evidence (read 16:04): `engine.ts:2901-2904` — `catch { /* keyed synthesis unavailable → deterministic floor stands */ return floor; }`. Codegen refusals already land `p6.synthesis.refused` rows (coded), but an import/transport THROW after the key probe returns true degrades with zero evidence — exactly the class OV-13 (`:3288-3305`) exists to kill.
- Fix: keep the `configured` value; on catch when `configured === true` return `withLiveDegradeRows(floor, [liveDegradeRow(6, "synthesis", err, "RT_E_LLM_REJECTED")])`; add `"synthesis"` to `LiveDegradeKind` (`:3306`). Keyless/unconfigured passes stay byte-identical.
- Pin: extend the existing `liveDegradeRow` unit coverage (it is exported) for the new kind; the wiring itself is DI-hard (accepted limit).

**R1-f10 · P3 · `runP6Real`'s catch is silent (thrown runner ≡ not-configured). [NEW]**
- Evidence (read 16:09): `engine.ts:3449-3453` — `if (!result || reason === "not-configured") { return { ok:false, result:null } } catch { return { ok:false, result:null } }`. A runner throw is indistinguishable from "not configured"; caller `store.ts:1366-1400` (`runModuleReal`) only acts on `out.ok && out.result`, so the throw leaves the template verdict with no row.
- Fix: `catch (err) { return { ok:false, result:null, row: liveLayerFailureRow(6, err) } }` (exported helper, fits the G2 shape); `runModuleReal` appends `out.row` to the project logs when present (R2 coordinates the store half). Done-when: a fake runner that throws → the project carries a coded `p6.live.failed` row.

C1-f10 (four transition impls) not re-audited here (store.ts is R2's box; no new divergence seen in passing).
**R1 verified-clean (evidence read today):** `phases.ts` (230 lines) — pure defs + helpers only; `phaseDef` throws coded RT_E_PHASE_ILLEGAL (:119-121); `canEnter`/`priorsSealed` deny phantom ids (:126-148); `phaseCoverage` is id-matched first-wins and excludes packet rows (:195-218); both consumers wired (`packet.ts:381`, `:602`). ### §R13 — THE "BEST IN THE WORLD" GAP (competitive/leapfrog lens) — LANE R13
**Read window:** RapidTool anchors re-read 2026-09-26 ~15:55–16:15 CDT on HEAD `e3d3fe4`; competitor facts are
dossier-cited (`90_LEAPFROG.md`, `107`, `108`, `219e`, `219f`, `224`, `13` — clone revs pinned in `219f §0`).
**Lens:** the MISSION ("truly autonomous software factory / best harness in the world") vs what the field's best
*ship*. Ordered by mission value. Full designs + per-finding detail: `fixes/269_lane13_audit_gap.md`.
**Bottom line:** the field's best have five decisive capabilities RapidTool lacks (or holds below the bar):
**f01 unattended end-to-end runs** (the mission's core promise; blocked by A1-f01/A1-f02 + no scheduler),
**f02 plan-before-execute**, **f03 checkpoint/rollback/resume per step**, **f04 a real isolation boundary**
(BLOCKED: host), **f05 verification against the RUNNING product + emitted-app preview**. Then f06 MCP client ·
f07 streaming/steering · f08 allow-lists wired into the autonomy path · f09 provider failover/cross-family judge ·
f10 learning-loop measurement sliver · f11 run-budget invest semantics · f12 gate-review surface · f13 deploy/preview
node · f14 subagent fan-out (wire-or-de-scope) · f15 repo index at scale (DEFER).

**CLOSED SINCE THE DOSSIERS — verified today; do NOT re-fix:** CI (`gate.yml`, W17) · provider #2 `xai` +
`RAPIDTOOL_PROVIDER` (`registry.ts:119/:184/:206-220`) · policy seam (`policy.ts:207`) · `patch.ts` + `edit` verb
(`patch.ts:279`; CLI `:1321`) · cassette T5.7 (`cassette.ts`; `io.ts:53-55`) · chat persistence + context meter
(`types.ts:402`; console PB-05b `:5717-5723`) · lesson decay/retire/supersede + promotion gate (`knowledge.ts`) ·
`eval` per-slug trend (`rapidtool-cli.mjs:1009-1024`) · emitted-tree install+declared-build verdicts
(`compile-check.server.ts:908/:1148/:1377`) · P6 lesson injection · test density ~0.49 (leads the clones' 0.13–0.29).

**R13-f01 · autonomy/unattended runs · P0 · READY (engine edges spec'd in §A1)** — The unattended end-to-end run
does not exist. Today: `wildStep` = the autonomy seam (`engine.ts:3820`); console driver cap-20 park
(`store.ts:1124`); CLI `wild` = start|pause|resume|stop only (`rapidtool-cli.mjs:2842`); **no
scheduler/daemon/queue** (`grep -rniE "scheduler|daemon|cron" src/lib scripts/rapidtool-cli.mjs` → only the
constraint-scheduler algorithm family). Structural blockers already in-log: **A1-f01** (wild never calls keyed P6
synthesis) + **A1-f02** (chunked mint unreachable outside eval/repair). Acceptance evidence: keyed 4/9
(`research/eval-out/_keyed-run.log`, 2026-09-10), keyless 6/9 — "no mode has ever demonstrated substance + runtime
+ machine verification together" (`219e §3.1`). Field: Devin fleets/team-of-Devin (`90 §1.4`); Copilot cloud agent
research→plan→branch→PR (`90 §1.14`); OpenHands automations own *when* via cron triggers (`107 §5` Q8
`automation-service.api.test.ts:71-74`). Fix: (a) land A1-f01/f02's specs; (b) `wild --until` / `--unattended`
policy path routing every machine decision through `policy.ts` (f08) + run cap (f11); (c) a dumb `auto` entry
(external cron/queue hook — no in-product daemon on this host). Done-when: keyed non-stock-gate project completes
`wild --start --unattended` with ≥1 minted `tested` tree-run, zero human touches on eligible gates, ledger row per
auto decision, one honest `eval` row. Mission: this IS the mission.

**R13-f02 · plan-first · P1 · READY** — No plan-before-execute. Today: `grep -n "wild.plan\|autoPlan"
src/lib/engine.ts src/lib/store.ts` → **0**; Wild executes then reviews (`wildApproveGate` `engine.ts:3641`);
`planning.ts` exists, consumer = `crew.server.ts` only (`90 §10.1` P0-4/P1-6). Field: Codex `/plan` approval
(`90 §1.1`); Cursor Plan Mode — reviewable plan files (`90 §1.3`); Antigravity plan Artifact + Proceed/Review
(`90 §1.11`); Copilot plan-as-artifact (`90 §1.14`); Dyad plan mode + approval (`108 §8` Q1). Fix = `90 §10.1`
P0-4 + P1-6 (one change set): `wild.plan` pre-execute from `planning.ts`'s step shape; classifier reviews the plan;
pause unless `autoPlan`/policy; plan rows on `Project`; render in packet + dock. Done-when: no execution without
approved plan or explicit auto-plan policy; packet carries plan + DoD criteria; RED-first pause test. Mission:
controls spend/correctness before the wrong artifact exists (the 86/87/88 class).

**R13-f03 · checkpoint/rollback/resume · P1 · READY** — No per-step checkpoint, no undo, no run resume. Today: no
checkpoint subsystem (`grep -rliE "checkpoint" src/lib/` → prose only in algorithms/dod/phases/scaffold);
`Project` has no checkpoints field; no `resume` verb (CLI `"resume"` = the wild pause action, `:2873`); ledger
append-only but irreversible. Field: Gemini shadow-git + `/restore` re-proposes the tool call (`90 §1.10`); Cline
checkpoint-diff/restore + hooks (`107 §4` Q2); OpenCode `Snapshot{capture|files|diff|preview|restore}` +
