### §R8 — THE MISSION-GAP REGISTER (LANE R8 · W27 THE MISSION-GAP ANALYST) — the definitive ordered list of capabilities/end-to-end flows still missing or broken for RapidTool to be a truly autonomous software factory

**Lane log:** `fixes/267_lane8_audit_mission.md` · **HEAD:** `e3d3fe4` · **Read window:** this session (2026-09-27); every anchor read on THIS tree today unless marked "W25 anchor". **Rules honored:** no product edits, no keyed calls (account unfunded), no full-gate runs. **Inputs:** §THE MISSION above + A1–A4/C1–C6 + the W24–W26 ledger + `research/eval-out/**` + canon rows (`220_tasklist_canonical.md:85-188`, `fixes/228_ledger_and_risk_register.md` §3) + W17–W26 landing logs. **Ordering rule:** what unlocks the most mission value first — T0 the spine ("a keyed run completes with LLM-authored, machine-verified code"), T1 flow completion (checkpoint · streaming · cassette · allow-lists · seat · universality), T2 breadth + acceptance + honesty residuals.

**HEADLINE.** Four W25-era mission gaps have **MOVED** — they are half-landed, not absent (see §R8-UPDATES): T5.7 cassette (module built, zero consumers), F4 allow-lists (overlay core, no engine consult), F2 second family (xai candidate + probe + env selection; no live mint, no failover), T5.1 streaming (lib leg + scripts test; no user surface). The **two P0 spine findings STAND unchanged**: `wildStep` never touches the model, and the "tested" mint is reachable only from `eval`/`repair`. Until those two land, **no verb a user or agent actually runs** (`run`, `gate`, `wild`, the console) can produce an LLM-authored, machine-verified tree — the mission's core loop is structurally unreachable outside the benchmark driver. That is #1 and #2.

**T0 — THE SPINE**

**R8-f01 · ONE keyed journey (convergence: wild vs live vs console vs eval) · P0 ·**
- **Today:** `wildStep` (engine.ts:3820) executes with `applyPhaseResult(running, phase)` ONLY (engine.ts:3986); `upgradeP6Keyed` (engine.ts:2869) — the only keyed path to `codegen liveP6Synthesis` — has exactly ONE caller, `liveLayer` (engine.ts:2920); the engine's own section comment claiming the seam is "shared by liveLayer AND the wild P6 step — ONE seam for both faces" (engine.ts:2853-2856) is drift. `wildStartRun` (headless.ts:3759) and the console wild driver (`store.ts` `startWildRun`) loop `wildStep`. The console's ONE journey face — `store.runP6Journey` (store.ts:925-966 → `headless.driveP6Journey`) — has ZERO UI callers (`grep -rn runP6Journey src/components src/routes` → empty). Console-created projects are never `enforceDod=true` (engine.ts:1902-1903 defaults via a `process.env` probe that is false in the browser, engine.ts:1814-1826; `store.create` store.ts:1164-1173 passes no override).
- **Missing (exact):** the keyed edge in `wildStep`; a UI control invoking the one journey face; the keyed default honestly reachable in the app.
- **Smallest complete design:** (a) wildStep's P6 execution edge, when keyed → `done = await runPhaseAsync(running, 6)` (engine.ts:3400 — the same seam `liveLayer` uses: ONE code path), then the mint step of R8-f02; (b) console P6 Execute → `store.runP6Journey` (or delete the mirror and route the button through runPhaseAsync + mint); (c) thread `llmStatus.configured` into `store.create`.
- **Done-when:** funded: `wild --start` on a webapp brief logs `p6.synthesis.attempt` + lands a generatedTree + `p6-verified` PASS → P7 reachable without `RT_E_GATE_PENDING`; `wild-step.test.ts` with an injected live seam asserts keyed P6 invokes it.

**R8-f02 · Mint reachability · P0 ·**
- **Today:** chunked trees null `lastTest` by design (codegen.server.ts:3383, `treeUpgraded` :3323-3399); the only re-mint is `mintTreeRunTested` (:3450) via `applyTreeRunTestedMint` (headless.ts:1962), called ONLY at headless.ts:2100/:2142 (`driveP6Journey`) and :2687/:2699 (`repairTreeDir`). `runUntilGate` (headless.ts:597) → `advanceOnce` (:511) → `runPhase` (:350)/`liveLayer` never tree-run or mint; CLI `run`/`gate` call `runUntilGate` (rapidtool-cli.mjs:467-537); the CLI never calls `driveP6Journey`. `p6-verified` (dod.ts:583-601) requires `lastTest.label==="tested"` + evidenceHash. Committed evidence: `results.json` `"stall":"P6 DoD unmet — hard gate blocks advance: p6-verified, p6-brief-fit."`, `stopCode:"RT_E_GATE_PENDING"`.
- **Missing (exact):** the tree-run → mint → gate-record step at the run-path P6 edge.
- **Smallest complete design:** one helper (mint-after-live-P6): when a live P6 landed `generatedTree` and `p6-verified` is unmet → emit candidate into a clean temp dir (`prepareCleanEmitDir` semantics, run-eval.mjs:783) → `treeRun(dir)` → on green `applyTreeRunTestedMint` + `recordGateInto`; call from `runPhase` and `wildStep`; surface the verdict in the summary.

Box: `src/lib/llm/**` (llm.ts facade, providers/{deepseek,openai-compatible,registry,probe}.ts), `deepseek.server.ts`, `codegen.server.ts` (budget/synthesis seam), `verify.server.ts` (budget seam), `headless.ts` (verify seam), `rt-events.ts`, `rt-ring.ts`, `rt-client.ts`, `rt-timeline.ts`, `watchdog.ts`, `diagnose.ts`, `errors.ts`, `runtime-log.server.ts`, the committed H3/H1 probes. **R4-f01 · PROVIDER (openai-compatible/xAI) · P1 — a broke wallet still reads as a generic refusal (B3-f02 CONFIRMED UNFIXED at HEAD; exact fix + live re-proof).**
- Evidence (read today): `src/lib/llm/providers/openai-compatible.ts:488-495` — the `!res.ok` branch returns `code: "RT_E_LLM_REJECTED"` unconditionally, even though `describeError` already prints the balance class (`:335-357`; the 402 case at `:340-341`). DeepSeek's W23-L1 doctrine (`deepseek.ts:437-444`) maps exactly this class to `RT_E_LLM_NO_BALANCE` (status 402 OR `/insufficient balance/i` on the refusal text; message byte-identical).
- Live re-proof (ran today, keyless — injected fake fetch + dummy keys): `node --experimental-strip-types probe-h3-balance.ts` → `DeepSeek 402  → code: RT_E_LLM_NO_BALANCE` · `xAI     402  → code: RT_E_LLM_REJECTED` · message `xAI account balance exhausted (HTTP 402: Insufficient Balance) — requested model: "probe-model-id"`. The message says balance-exhausted; the code says generic refusal.
- Why vs mission: `ERROR_CODE_DOC` drives agent action (`errors.ts:398-408`): REJECTED → "check the key or the parameters"; NO_BALANCE → "stop consuming this account … top up and re-run". A broke xAI wallet through this adapter teaches an agent the wrong next action; the wallet-class doctrine (fixes/262 / W23-L1) is provider-wide, not DeepSeek-only.
- Fix (mirror `deepseek.ts:437-444` exactly): in the `!res.ok` branch — `const refusalText = await describeError(spec.label, res); const noBalance = res.status === 402 || /insufficient balance/i.test(refusalText); return { ok: false, reason: "error", error: `${refusalText} — requested model: "${model}"${exhausted}`, code: noBalance ? "RT_E_LLM_NO_BALANCE" : "RT_E_LLM_REJECTED" };` — plus a one-line BROADENING of `errors.ts:403-408`'s doc string ("DeepSeek HTTP 402" → "the provider's HTTP 402 / \"Insufficient Balance\"") once a second family maps in.
- Verify (done-when): (a) add a `registry.test.ts` case — fake fetch → 402 + `{error:{message:"Insufficient Balance"}}` through `createOpenAiCompatibleProvider(XAI_SPEC, ["probe-model-id"])` → code `RT_E_LLM_NO_BALANCE`; (b) re-run `probe-h3-balance.ts` → BOTH lines read `RT_E_LLM_NO_BALANCE`.

**R4-f02 · TELEMETRY (call tap) · B3-f01 RE-CHECK — fix CONFIRMED landed and contained; the missing piece is an ASYNC regression test.**
- Fix read (current bytes): `deepseek.ts:585-607` — `tapLlmCall` wraps the dispatch in try/catch AND contains the promise channel (`then(undefined, …)` on any thenable return; the comment cites the W26 H3 repro). The wrapper (`:614-630`) still forwards results untouched; no tap installed ⇒ byte-identical path.
- Re-check executed today (keyless, zero network): (1) `node --experimental-strip-types probe-h3-tap.ts` → `case1 sync-throwing tap → call survived: true code: RT_E_LLM_NOT_CONFIGURED` · `case2 async-rejecting tap → call survived: true code: RT_E_LLM_NOT_CONFIGURED` · `case2 unhandledRejection fired: []` · `VERDICT: contained` (EXIT=0); (2) `probe-h3-tap-crash.ts` (no rejection listener — simulates a real CLI's default throw) → `call survived: true` · `REACHED-END (process did not crash)` (EXIT=0); (3) `node --experimental-strip-types --test --test-name-pattern="W24/B2" scripts/run-eval.test.mjs` → `# tests 4 # pass 4 # fail 0`.
- GAP: the committed test (`run-eval.test.mjs:1029-1047`) installs a SYNC-throwing tap only — nothing pins the promise channel, so a future refactor can silently drop the `.then(undefined, …)` containment (the probe would catch it, but root probes are in NO test glob: `package.json` legs cover `scripts/**/*.test.mjs` + `src/lib/**/*.test.ts`).
- Spec (done-when): add the async twin beside it — install `ds.setLlmCallTap(async () => { throw new Error("async tap boom"); })`; keyless `await ds.completeDeepSeek("…")` must return `RT_E_LLM_NOT_CONFIGURED`; then `await new Promise((r) => setImmediate(r))` so a floating rejection can surface (node:test fails the test on an unhandled rejection → RED without the containment, GREEN with it); restore `setLlmCallTap(null)` in `finally`.

**R4-f03 · BUDGET LEDGER · P2 — A2-f07 spec: the "session cap" that isn't; the smallest complete process-scoped design.**
- Evidence (read today): `codegen.server.ts:2310-2317` — `synthWithBackoff(complete, prompt, opts, sleep, ledger = emptyLedger(), cap = p6TokenCapFromEnv())`; BOTH production call sites pass 3 args (`:2817` single-module, `:3174` per-chunk) → a FRESH ledger per call → `RAPIDTOOL_TOKEN_CAP` can only trip when ONE call's own usage exceeds the whole cap; N calls never accumulate. `p6TokenCapFromEnv` (`:2282-2288`) duplicates `tokenCapFromEnv` (`token-ledger.ts:125-132`). The T-107.6 seam is fully built but UNWIRED in production: `createTokenBudget` (`token-ledger.ts:298-303`) has ZERO production callers (repo grep — only `verify.server.test.ts:711`); `verify.server.ts`'s budget param (`:791-797` generateCases 6th arg; `:946-967` `verifyAndFixServer` `opts.budget`; `:986` threading) is never supplied — `headless.ts:3025-3027` (`verifyGeneratedSource`) calls `verifyAndFixServer(source, brief)` with no opts.
- Design (mirrors T-107.6's own "ONE caller-owned ledger threaded through seams", scoped to the process): (1) `token-ledger.ts`: add a lazily-created module singleton `sessionTokenBudget(): TokenBudget` (`{ ledger: emptyLedger(), cap: tokenCapFromEnv() }`) + `resetSessionBudget()` for tests/an explicit new-session boundary; harden `tokenCapFromEnv`'s default for non-Node hosts (`typeof process === "undefined"` ⇒ unmetered). (2) `codegen.server.ts`: defaults become `ledger = sessionTokenBudget().ledger`, `cap = sessionTokenBudget().cap`; DELETE `p6TokenCapFromEnv` (one env parser). (3) `headless.ts` `verifyGeneratedSource`: pass `{ budget: sessionTokenBudget() }` as `verifyAndFixServer`'s 3rd arg — the existing `runBudgetedCall` gates + records; a stop is already the coded `RT_E_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED` refusal. (4) Document the semantic: one cap per PROCESS (CLI run = one process; dev server = one server session); spend is kept, never clamped (existing `accountSynthUsage` mutates in place).
- Verify (done-when): with `RAPIDTOOL_TOKEN_CAP=1000` and a stub complete reporting 600 tokens/call, TWO sequential `synthWithBackoff` calls refuse on the second (RED today: both pass); a budgeted verify pass records into the same ledger; typecheck/lint 0. If the owner prefers per-call semantics, the alternative is renaming the env var's docs — but T-107.6's "budgets that bind" argues for the singleton.

**R4-f04 · FACADE TYPE GAP · P3 — C1-f08 CONFIRMED.**
- Evidence: `llm.ts:77-84` — exported `complete()`'s opts omit `thinking`, while its own server-fn validator declares it (`:47-63`) and forwards it verbatim (`:65-68`); that validator's doc says "The facade must forward it or the UI / engine path silently re-enables that blocker" (`:56-61`). Runtime works today only because `llm-tasks.ts:45-61` defines its own 5-field `CompleteOptions` + `Completer` and structure passes the extra key through; a direct `complete()` caller gets a TS error for the one knob the pro-tier json path needs.
- Fix: add the documented `thinking?: boolean` member to `complete()`'s public opts type (same doc as the validator).
- Verify: typecheck-only — a scratch caller `complete(p, { thinking: false })` compiles; no runtime change (the value already forwards).

**R4-f05 · MODEL-ID VALIDATION · P3 — C1-f09 CONFIRMED + refined: the provider-side refusal must exist or the header claim is false.**
- Evidence: `deepseek.ts:343-347` — no validation: `model = opts.model ?? flash`; `isPro` decides defaults/timeouts; the id is then SENT verbatim (`body.model`, `:380-398`). The header's claim (`:49-51`: "unknown ids are refused before any network I/O, and this provider never falls back to a default") is enforced only in `registry.ts` (`checked`/`isKnownModelId` `:551-560`; explicit/env paths `:389-429`, `:303-361`). Bypass: `llm.ts complete()` → `completeServer` (validator echoes input; `model?: LlmModel` is TS-only) → `completeDeepSeek` — an out-of-set id reaches the wire and returns as a vendor 400 `RT_E_LLM_REJECTED` instead of a pre-network coded refusal. `registry.ts:24-28` documents that the status list (`deepSeekModelIds()`) intentionally carries the forward-compat `deepseek-v4-flash-vision-exp` that the explicit rule refuses — so the provider check must use THIS module's request list (`DEEPSEEK_MODEL_IDS`, incl. vision-exp), not the registry's narrower verified set.
- Fix: in `completeDeepSeekInner`, after resolving `model`, refuse unknown ids pre-network — `if (!DEEPSEEK_MODEL_IDS.includes(model)) return { ok: false, reason: "error", error: 'DeepSeek cannot request model "<id>" — known request ids: …', code: "RT_E_VALIDATION" };` (keep the `?? flash` default; type stays `LlmModel`).
- Verify: new `deepseek.server.test.ts` case — unknown id + fetch spy → `RT_E_VALIDATION`, fetch NOT called; the existing 21 tests stay green (all use known ids).

**R4-f06 · TELEMETRY PURITY · P2 — a poisoned crash snapshot can throw the OBSERVER (diagnose builder); C1-f01's sibling.**
- Evidence chain (read today): `rt-client.ts:264` accepts any numeric `parsed.at` from the localStorage crash blob (no finiteness check); `:266` assigns `crashSnapshot = { at, href }` BEFORE `:273` runs `new Date(at).toISOString()` inside a template string; a JSON blob `{"at":1e999}` parses to `Infinity` (ran: `node -e 'const at = JSON.parse("{\"at\":1e999}").at; …'` → `parsed at: Infinity finite: false` · `THROWS: RangeError Invalid time value`). The `:273` throw is swallowed ("corrupt snapshot — drop it silently"), but the poisoned `crashSnapshot` SURVIVES; `buildClientDiagnosis` (`:624-696`, passes it at `:695`) → `diagnose.ts:281` `new Date(crashSnapshot.at).toISOString()` in the `freeze-pre-capture` summary (rule fires whenever `crashSnapshot` is non-null, `:277-284`) → RangeError escapes the Runtime Log pane's diagnosis builder.
- Fix: (a) `rt-client.ts:264` → `const at = typeof parsed.at === "number" && Number.isFinite(parsed.at) ? parsed.at : Date.now();`; (b) belt-and-braces at `diagnose.ts:281` → `Number.isFinite(crashSnapshot.at) ? new Date(crashSnapshot.at).toISOString() : "an unknown time"`.
- Verify: (a) unit test — `buildDiagnosis({ …, crashSnapshot: { at: Infinity, href: "" } })` must not throw; (b) rt-client hydrate test with a `{"at":1e999}` blob → finite `crashSnapshot.at` (or the null path) and no swallowed throw.

**R4-f07 · TELEMETRY PURITY · P3 — `rtHydrate` bypasses the ring's own caps.**
- Evidence: `rt-ring.ts:182-236` — hydrate appends EVERY well-formed row from the blob; `RING_CAP`/`RING_BYTES` are enforced only on push (`:135-146`). localStorage is user-writable; an oversized blob loads unbounded rows into memory on every page load (rtStats/rtTail/diagnose then iterate them).
- Fix: enforce the cap at hydrate — `rows.slice(-RING_CAP)` after the loop (or push each row through `rtPush` so the byte cap applies too).
- Verify: hydrate a 10k-row blob → `rows.length ≤ 200`.

**R4-f08 · IMPORT BOUNDARY · P3 — `node:` map for non-`.server.ts` modules; `knowledge.ts` is the one genuinely inside the client graph.**
- (a) `knowledge.ts:27-28` statically imports `node:fs`/`node:path` and is NOT `.server.ts`; it IS in the client graph — `engine.ts:97` `void import("./knowledge.ts").then(…).catch(…)` (module-scope, runs on the client) + `report-intake.ts:63` same pattern. The null-fallback is load-bearing and every use site is null-guarded (`engine.ts:549, 651, 1468`); only the PURE ranking helpers are read (the fs-backed `KnowledgeStore` stays server-side in practice). But the invariant ("never touch fs at module scope; the client never calls the store") is undocumented in the file header and untested.
- (b) `write-set.ts:14-15` (node:fs/node:path) and `ast-mine.ts:23` (node:path) also lack the `.server.ts` suffix; grep-verified today: ZERO non-test static importers outside server modules (`headless.ts` only, dynamic at `:3565`/`:3588-3595`).
- (c) Verified-clean boundaries: `headless.ts` — zero static non-test importers repo-wide; `store.ts:925-937` assembles the specifier at runtime (`"./headless." + "ts"`) with an honest browser refusal (same idiom as engine's server probes); `deepseek.server.ts`'s re-export chain carries no `node:` imports.
- Fix: add the explicit header contract to `knowledge.ts` / `write-set.ts` / `ast-mine.ts` ("server-only graph; the client may import ONLY the pure helpers and must never call fs-backed methods; no module-scope fs use") + a guard test (honesty-guards-style source scan, or an import test asserting the module evaluates without touching disk). Optional later wave: rename to `*.server.ts` with import-site updates.
- Verify: the guard test is RED if a module-scope fs call is added; build/typecheck stay green.

**R4-f09 · PROVIDER PROBE · P4 — `probe.ts`'s "never throws" doc has one reachable throw.**
- Evidence: `probe.ts:113` `const probedAt = new Date((opts.now ?? Date.now)()).toISOString();` runs BEFORE the try (`:154`); a hostile/broken injected clock (`opts.now: () => NaN | Infinity`) throws `RangeError` out of a function documented "Never throws; never caches; never invents an id" (`:104-107`). Only the test/dev hook can trigger it, but this function is the CLI probe surface.
- Fix: validate the hook result — `const nowMs = (opts.now ?? Date.now)(); const probedAt = Number.isFinite(nowMs) ? new Date(nowMs).toISOString() : new Date().toISOString();`.
- Verify: unit test with `now: () => NaN` → resolves with a result object, no throw.

**R5-f01 · gate.sh · P1 — A2-f02 CONFIRMED + REPRODUCED LIVE: a green verdict with a red test leg; exit-condition fix + the fixture that proves it.**
- Evidence (read today): `scripts/gate.sh:175-178` — the verdict's exit condition gates only `gates.typecheck.ok && gates.lint.ok && gates.assertionGaps.ok && gates.build.ok`; the two test legs are recorded (`:154-173` builds `gates.testsScripts/testsLib` with `ok: exitOf(id)===0`) but never consulted, and `hardFails` is populated ONLY from TAP `not ok` names (`:139-142`, `:165`, `:171`). A leg that exits non-zero WITHOUT a TAP line (runner crash, unresolvable glob, missing file, OOM) → `hardFails` empty, `flakes` empty, other gates green → `ok:true`, exit 0.
- REPRO (ran today, real output): extracted the embedded verdict program byte-for-byte from gate.sh (`<<'NODE' … NODE`, 2,859 chars) and ran it with fixture results where `testsScripts.exit=1` and its log carries ZERO `not ok` lines → stdout `{"ok":true,…,"hardFails":[],"gates":{…,"testsScripts":{"ok":false,"exit":1,…}}}` and **exit 0**. Control fixture (same exits, log has `not ok 1 - some failing test` + `# fail 1`) → `hardFails:["some failing test"]`, `ok:false`, exit 1. So the bug window is precisely the no-TAP-line class, and the fix must close it.
- Fix (patch shape): `gate.sh:178` →
  `else if (!(gates.typecheck.ok && gates.lint.ok && gates.assertionGaps.ok && gates.testsScripts.ok && gates.testsLib.ok && gates.build.ok)) code = 1;`
  Recommended companion (diagnostic honesty): inside the leg loop (`:154-173`), when `exitOf(id) !== 0 && run1.failNames.length === 0` (and, under `--double`, run2 produced none either) push a synthetic `hardFails` entry `` `${id} (exit ${exitOf(id)} — runner produced no TAP failure lines; read the gate log tail)` `` so the verdict is loud instead of nameless. Deliberately NOT a flake: a runner-level failure has no test name to compare, and `fixes/173` §F-H11 forbids classifying a red as a flake (`--double` run-1-crash/run-2-clean must still read exit 1, never 3).
- Fixture (the proof; new file): `scripts/gate-verdict.test.mjs` — (1) read `scripts/gate.sh`; extract the heredoc between `<<'NODE'` and a line `NODE` (fail the test loudly if the markers move); (2) write the four fixtures below into a temp dir; (3) invoke via `spawnSync(process.execPath, ["-", results, startMs, double, tclog, lintlog, s1, l1, s2, l2, bldlog], { input: program })`; (4) assert: **A** (leg exit 1, zero `not ok`, others 0) → `ok:false` + exit 1 *[RED at HEAD — this IS the P1 proof]*; **B** (leg exit 1 + `not ok`) → `hardFails` names it, exit 1; **C** (all 0, `# fail 0`) → `ok:true` exit 0; **D** (`--double`, run1 names X / run2 names Y) → exit 3, `flakes` ⊇ {X,Y}; pass `""` for the second-run log args when not doubled.
- Verify: `node --test scripts/gate-verdict.test.mjs` (fixture A red before the patch, green after); `npm test` scripts leg stays green; done-when: ANY non-zero leg exit forces `ok:false`/exit≠0.

"human" : "autopilot" })` and persist the stamp (see R5-f09). **R5-f03 · exit taxonomy · P2 — A2-f03 CONFIRMED + WIDENED: FOURTEEN verbs answer `RT_E_INTERNAL` on exit 1; spec the conforming exit + a mechanical guard.**
- Evidence (read today; script-scanned every `errCode(err, "RT_E_INTERNAL")` site and its following exit arg): exit **1** at `packet:771`, `emit:939`, `fixtures:1002`, `vault:1651`, `search:1669`, `design:1835`, `diagram:1870`, `harvest:1914`, `swarm:1939`, `bench:1989`, `knowledge:2085`, `lesson:2827`, `wild:2905`, `deepen:3020`. The contract says internal = **4**: README-cli.md:858 (“Exit 4 = backend or internal runtime failure”), error table `:910` (`RT_E_INTERNAL` … typical exit 4), the pillar block's OWN comment `rapidtool-cli.mjs:1884-1888` (“unexpected module failure → outer handler (4)”), and the outer handler itself (`:3145-3153`, exit 4). The drift's origin: `:1649` (“Missing vault module / write failure → clean exit 1 (documented)”) conflates the documented optional-module-absent case with real throws in one catch; agents branching on `exit_code` read internal failures as usage errors.
- Fix (patch shape): at each of the 14 sites change the respond exit argument `1` → `4` (the code stays whatever `errCode` classifies; a true internal throw now exits 4). Also assert README-cli.md still carries the “Exit 4 = backend or internal runtime failure” sentence.
- Verify: `node --test scripts/cli-exit-taxonomy.test.mjs`; spot behavior: `node … vault --project <nonexistent>` unchanged (loadProjectFor path, exit 1 `RT_E_VALIDATION`/`RT_E_PROJECT_MISSING` — do not disturb).

**R5-f12 · human-seat token · P2 — C2-f02 CONFIRMED at HEAD + the two-channel spec.**
- Evidence (read today): `rapidtool-cli.mjs:604-605` (approve) and `:1425-1426` (edit) pass `humanSeatToken` AND `expectedHumanSeatToken` from the SAME `process.env.RT_HUMAN_SEAT_TOKEN` read → `isVerifiedHumanSeatToken` (`policy.ts:171-180`) collapses to “the env var is non-empty”; the `human-seat-unverified` denies (`policy.ts:237-240`, `:267-270`) are unreachable from both CLI sites; `headless.ts:867-871` keeps the expected side env-sourced while the presented side is `opts.humanSeatToken` — so any process that simply exports the var (its own value!) passes. `README-cli.md:1039-1042` claims “a process that merely inherited an agent's environment does not hold it” — false as implemented, and worse: the check never compares against any human-known reference. Tests pinning today's behavior: `rapidtool-cli-edit.test.mjs:300-330` (deny without token) and `:401-427` (ALLOW, non-TTY, token via child env) — both must be re-pointed with whichever track lands.
- Two-channel design (patch shape, TRACK 1 — recommended):
  1. **Channel A (possession/claim)** stays `RT_HUMAN_SEAT_TOKEN` in the environment (the out-of-band secret the human exports).
  2. **Channel B (presence/confirmation)** = an interactive re-entry the environment cannot furnish: on `approve --i-am-human` / `edit --write --i-am-human` with `process.stdin.isTTY === true`, prompt once (`Confirm human seat — re-enter RT_HUMAN_SEAT_TOKEN:`) and pass the TYPED value as `humanSeatToken` while `expectedHumanSeatToken` stays the env read (`policy.ts` remains pure; the prompt lives in the CLI). Mismatch/EOF → the seam's `human-seat-unverified` deny → exit 1 `RT_E_VALIDATION`, nothing written. This KILLS the same-read degeneracy and makes the deny branch reachable.
  3. **Non-TTY** (`--i-am-human` from an agent): REFUSE the human seat by default (exit 1, message pointing at the autopilot gate) — no second channel exists there, so no human row may be minted; if the owner instead keeps headless human approvals, take TRACK 2 as a stopgap.
  4. Rewrite `README-cli.md` §The `approve` seat (`:1008-1042`) to the two-channel contract + the honest ceiling: same-OS-user processes can always read env/files — the seat is an **accountability control under the ledger-trust assumption, not a security boundary**; the check must not be VACUOUS, and every kept path's claim must be true.
  - TRACK 2 (minimum if interactivity is descoped): keep the code; declare-or-strike the docs — `policy.ts:165-180` + README `:1033-1042` state “both sides are read from the caller's own environment, so the check proves only that a non-empty `RT_HUMAN_SEAT_TOKEN` exists; any process that can set its own environment can satisfy it.”
- Verify: seam unit coverage already exists (`policy.test.ts` VERIFIED/mismatch cases); add a static guard (can live in the R5-f03 test file): no CLI arm may pass the same `process.env.RT_HUMAN_SEAT_TOKEN` expression to BOTH fields (regex over the arm source); Track-1 TTY path: manual terminal run with a wrong re-entry → exit 1 `/human-seat-unverified/` (record it in the lane log; a TTY cannot be faked from the agent — that is the point).

(Scan verified against HEAD: exactly the 4 claim lines above match — no false positives.)

**R5-f14 · write-atomic.mjs descope · P3 — C2-f05 CONFIRMED: legacy scaffolding, zero live callers, test coupled to `.grok/`.**

**R15-f02 · AUTH/SCHEMA OPERABILITY · P2 · Enabling auth in this repo yields a schema-less DB: the only auth migration lives in `migrations/auth/` and BOTH migrators are non-recursive with nothing performing the copy-up.**
- Evidence (read 16:12–16:16): `git ls-files migrations` → `migrations/auth/0001_auth.sql` (the only migration); `scripts/migrate.mjs:15-20` `readdir(migrationsDir)` non-recursive → "no migrations — nothing to do"; `src/lib/db.ts:134-141` glob `/migrations/*.sql` + its own comment "does not descend, so the opt-in auth schema under `migrations/auth/` stays out"; `scripts/with-app-env.mjs:54-61` honors any `VITE_AUTH_ENABLED=true` override. `audit/03` §8.2-7 recorded exactly this ("one flag flip away from a schema-less auth DB") + proposed a startup assertion — still unimplemented.
- Why vs mission: following the documented auth-enable path produces runtime write failures with no coded (RT_E_*) preflight diagnosis — the opposite of a professional harness.
- Fix: (a) guard — when auth is on, assert a migration creating `"user"` is present/applied via `pendingMigrations` (in `db.ts` + `migrate.mjs`), else refuse with an RT_E code + the copy-up instruction; or (b) auto-copy `migrations/auth/0001_auth.sql` → `migrations/` when auth is enabled. Sub-item (audit/03-G, still open): `migrations/auth/0001_auth.sql:40-54` — `account` has no unique index on `("providerId","accountId")` (duplicate link rows under audit/03-F's race); fix = follow-up migration + constraint handling.
- Done-when: enabling auth with no root migration prints a coded refusal (or applies the schema); pinned in `migration-plan.test.mjs`; unique index lands via a new `0002_*.sql`. **READY**.

**R15-f03 · DEPENDENCY HYGIENE · P3 · ~30 declared production dependencies have zero import sites in `src`+`scripts`+`server` (17 unused `@radix-ui/*` + the shadcn/template set); `eslint-plugin-prettier` is declared but unused by the config.**

No undeclared imports found (only `virtual:grok-og-identity`, a Vite virtual module). Used-and-real: alert-dialog/dropdown-menu/tabs/tooltip Radix, `cmdk`, `jose`, `kysely`, `pg`, `better-auth`, `@electric-sql/pglite`, `gpt-tokenizer`, `zod`, `zustand`, `lucide-react`, `playwright` (browser-smoke).
- Why vs mission: "dependency hygiene (dev vs prod)" is explicitly in the R15 box; every unused dep is supply-chain surface + install/CI weight + audit noise. Behavior impact: none (bundlers prune).
- Fix: prune after one `npm ls <pkg>` sanity pass + build/test; decide `clsx` (test-only use).
- Done-when: the census reports 0 unused prod deps (or each survivor listed with a reason); `npm run build` + both test legs green. **READY**.

**R15-f04 · GATE COVERAGE · P2 · `npm run typecheck` cannot see the largest file in the repo: `tsconfig.json` includes only `src`+`server`, so all 40 `scripts/**` (incl. `rapidtool-cli.mjs` ~154 KB — the agent-facing contract) and the root probes are outside the program.**
- Evidence (read 16:09–16:18): `tsconfig.json:32-35` `"include": ["src","server"]`; `package.json:17` `"typecheck": "tsc --noEmit"`; only `scripts/migration-plan.mjs` + `scripts/sign-out-plan.mjs` carry `@ts-check` (grep 16:18). eslint covers those files but performs no type checking.
- Why vs mission: "typecheck 0" is a load-bearing quality claim (AGENTS.md: keep gates at 0) yet the 37-verb CLI — where the A2-f03/A2-f08 drift class lives — is untyped; "professional quality in EVERY file" wants the biggest file inside a real gate.
- Fix (staged): (1) keep extending `@ts-check` across the pure-logic script modules (2 files prove the pattern); (2) add `tsconfig.scripts.json` with `checkJs` for `scripts/**` and fold it into `gate.sh` step 1 once green; (3) probes leave the typecheck-irrelevant root (see R15-f07).
- Done-when: `tsc` fails on a deliberate type error inside `rapidtool-cli.mjs` (or README-cli explicitly declares the file out-of-gate). **READY** (multi-step).

**R15-f05 · PROCESS/FOOTGUN · P3 · Formatting is shipped-but-never-checked: no `.prettierignore`, no format step in any gate, `eslint-plugin-prettier` unused — and the shipped `npm run format` would rewrite the eval corpus + platform assets.**
- Evidence (read 16:09–16:19): `.prettierrc` exists; `package.json:22` `"format": "prettier --write ."`; `ls .prettierignore` → absent; `scripts/gate.sh` contains no prettier step (gates = typecheck → lint → assertion-gap → tests → build); `eslint.config.mjs:51-52` uses only `eslint-config-prettier`; `eslint.config.mjs:18-21` itself documents "research/eval-out/** … intentional scaffold `var` style" — prettier has no such exclusion.

- Done-when: `npx prettier --check src scripts` green; `npm run format` touches no eval/platform file. **READY**.

**R15-f10 · VERSIONING · P3 · The product has no version identity while its inner contracts are versioned: `package.json` has no `version`, no `engines`, and still carries the template's name.**
- Evidence (read 16:09): `grep '"version"' package.json` → exit 1 (absent); no `engines` (exit 1) though Node 22 is mandatory (AGENTS.md; CI pins 22.23.2 — this host matches, `node -v` → v22.23.2); `package.json:2` `"name": "app-builder-workspace"`; inner contracts ARE versioned: every CLI envelope carries `schema_version` (`rapidtool-cli.mjs:125`), `PACKET_VERSION = 3` (`src/lib/packet.ts:25`). No CHANGELOG, no `--version`.
- Why vs mission: every packet/handoff a factory emits is quoted across time; "which RapidTool built this" must be answerable; a Node-18 install currently succeeds silently and then fails.
- Fix: add `"version"` + `"engines": {"node": ">=22"}` (+ consider `engine-strict=true` in `.npmrc`); surface the version in `doctor`/`--help`; optional rename of `name` (check lockfile root name).
- Done-when: `doctor` prints a version; install on Node <22 warns/refuses. **READY**.

**R15-f11 · i18n / LOCALE BOUNDARY · P3 · Zero i18n infrastructure and zero locale awareness — and it is never declared as a boundary.**
- Evidence (grep 16:16): no i18n library in deps, no locale tables; `Intl\.` → 0 hits in `src/`; `date-fns` (declared) imported nowhere; console strings are hardcoded en-US; `src/lib/auth/popup.server.ts:140` `<html lang="en">`.

- Fix: one line in README's honest-limits block (optionally a 94/remainingwaves row): "UI and emitted apps are English-only today; brief-level locale support is future work."
- Done-when: the limit is stated where the other limits are. **READY (one-line)**.

**R15-f12 · ACCESSIBILITY GUARD · P3 · a11y work exists and holds on the main surfaces, but there is NO automated a11y check anywhere; the emitted UI and the install CSS remain uncovered.**
- Evidence (16:17–16:19): `console.tsx` carries 96 `aria-` attributes; `scripts/install-page.html` has `lang` + `aria-label` + `aria-hidden` patterns; the error boundary uses `role="alert"` (`src/lib/error-component.tsx`); popup page uses `lang="en"`. Against: `scripts/browser-smoke.mjs`/`browser-smoke-verdict.mjs` contain no axe/aria assertions; no `@axe-core/*` dep; `public/__grok/install/styles.css` has 0 `:focus`/`:focus-visible` rules (grep); emitted-app UI is a name-dump with no semantics by construction (A3-f07, cross-ref).
- Why vs mission: "perfect UI/UX" includes keyboard/screen-reader access; with no guard, a11y silently regresses (W24 fixed `aria-controls` by hand — nothing catches the next regression).
- Fix: add `@axe-core/playwright` + one a11y assertion pass to `browser-smoke.mjs` (or a new `scripts/a11y-smoke.mjs`), baselined like the body-text verdict; add focus-visible styles to the install CSS.
- Done-when: an injected aria regression fails the smoke; install CSS defines visible focus. **READY**.

### §R14 — FULL-STACK WIRING TRACE (PARENT-COMPLETED from lane R14's own closed summary; the append never landed — author hit the iteration cap mid-write. All read/verified today at HEAD e3d3fe4)

### §R3 — THE FACTORY OUTPUT PATH (PARENT-COMPLETED from lane R3's own closed summary; append never landed — cap mid-read. All probes ran today on HEAD e3d3fe4)
- **R3-f01 · P0 · A3-f01 CONFIRMED + FULLY EXPLAINED + REPAIR PROVEN.** Live repro over the stored project: emitted `src/generated/get-property.ts` is **byte-identical** to `generatedTree[0].source`; **9× `module.exports` survive**; **no barrel emitted**. Import in a `"type":"module"` tenant → **`TypeError: Cannot set properties of undefined (setting 'getProperty')`** — sharper than A3's ReferenceError: on Node 22 `module` resolves; the failing binding is `exports`; the codegen validator's `FORBIDDEN_SOFT` matches only bare `module`/`exports` tokens, so `module.exports` slips the gate. **Root cause:** the fix exists only at SYNTHESIS (`synthesizeChunkedTree:3135`/`partialTreeRefuse:3297` call `materializeImportableTree`); the EMIT path never does; `emitProject` is the single chokepoint (run-eval/journey/repair all mutate `generatedTree` directly, so patching store create/hydrate would miss them); the verbatim write is `scaffold.ts:2543-2545`. **PROVEN REPAIR:** in `headless.ts:1584` `emitProject` (after `scaffoldProject`, before `:1595`), feed accepted rows through `materializeImportableTree` and replace the bytes when they differ → output `['src/generated.ts','src/generated/get-property.ts']`, **0 CJS refs, all 9 exports import, the barrel reproduces `generatedSource`**. Constraints: (a) `GeneratedTreeFile` stores no `exports` list → call with `exports: []` (neutralizeResidualCjsExports alone carries the surface — proven); (b) apply only when normalized ≠ stored (else `scaffold.test.ts:870`/"verbatim" + the 2-file fixture break); (c) client-safety — `codegen.server.ts` must NOT be statically imported by scaffold (helper in headless or a dynamic seam like `parseGateTreeRows:2506-2526`); (d) consequence: chunk-0 relocates `src/generated.ts → src/generated/chunk-0.ts` → tree-mode tests shift; (e) the `.rapidtool/project.json` record (scaffold.ts:2521) still carries UN-materialized bytes → doctor's `classifyJobSource` censuses stale CJS → **materialize the exported record too**; (f) pins: emit-twice byte-stable; RED-first 0 `module.exports` + a real `node --test` import smoke.
- **§R3-f01 AMENDMENT (E1's blocking discoveries, 17:33 — adopted; E1 probed this live):** (1) **`materializeImportableTree` is NOT idempotent** — recompute-and-compare (`normalized ≠ stored`) is the WRONG guard and would re-rewrite endlessly; **the guard must be CJS-residue-based** (apply iff the bytes contain CJS residue — scan `module.exports`/`exports.` before materializing; the residue is the signal, not a byte-diff). (2) The `generatedTree` record fields (`toGeneratedTreeFile` `codegen.server.ts:1673` + the `fnv1a` hash/`attempt` at `:96`) must be recomputed consistently for any materialized row, or the stored record disagrees with the emitted bytes. W-03's shape above stands otherwise.
- **R3-f02 · P1 · prompt vs emitted tsconfig (A3-f02 confirmed).** Independent run: `tsc --noEmit` over today's materialized output → exactly **15× TS7006**; **TS2306 is cured by the P0 fix**. Spec: reword `p6SynthesisSystemPrompt` rule 2 (`codegen.server.ts:465,482`) to **ESM named exports** + prompt-level param annotations (or documented noImplicitAny caveat) — `npm run typecheck` is README-advertised, so the artifact must be green.
- **R3-f03 · P1 · partial-synthesis semantics.** `partialTreeRefuse:3289` keeps prior chunks + sets `generatedSource` but **never nulls `lastTest`** (contrast `treeUpgraded:3383`). A3-f03's 28 reds are NOT the CJS bug: with a 1-file partial tree, `capScanPaths` (`scaffold.ts:1296-1304`) can't carry capabilities 9–36. Spec: (a) null `lastTest` on partial; (b) a machine-readable `redTree`/`partial` field on the emit envelope; (c) `capability.N` rows **skip-with-reason** when their module was never materialised — never a red that reads as product failure.
- **R3-f06 · P1 · kind coverage — EXACT gaps.** game declares `build:"vite build"` (`:560-566`) but `vite.config.ts` is pushed only for webapp (`:2529`); cli-tool/library/service emit only a throwing `main()` (`:936-962`) with **no package.json `main`/`bin`/`exports`/`files`**, and the emitted entry never calls `main`; service declares `PORT` with no wiring. Spec: per-kind entry wiring + package.json fields + one fixture per kind.
- **R3-f10 · DV-04 stale; TA-21 CLOSED.** `DV-04` has no home in the repo (0 grep hits; no git history) — legacy brief item only. TA-21's marker is present (`engine.ts:1031,1053`, pinned by `emitted-honesty.test.ts:115`) → **CLOSED-BY-PRIOR-WAVE; no coder work owed.**

### §R11 — SECURITY · SAFETY · ROBUSTNESS (PARENT-COMPLETED from lane R11's own closed summary; append never landed. All read-evidenced at HEAD e3d3fe4)
- **R11-f01 · P1 · the dev server is an unauthenticated remote-code-execution surface.** `vite.config.ts:150` binds **`0.0.0.0:8080`**; **ZERO** server functions carry `.middleware([authMiddleware])` (grep → only a doc comment `auth/middleware.ts:15`); TanStack's CSRF middleware is intentionally not installed (correct for the cross-origin iframe), and the server-fn handler parses `application/json` regardless of Origin (`start-server-core …server-functions-handler.js:58`). **Exploit:** any LAN host POSTs `/_serverFn/<id>` JSON → `exec.ts:41-56` → `runNodeBounded` → arbitrary `node -e`. Fix: bind loopback by default (or install the auth middleware / an Origin gate on `/_serverFn/*`); document the LAN exposure if deliberate.
- **R11-f02 · P1 · emitted trees are executed with `shell:true` and NO `--ignore-scripts`.** `compile-check.server.ts:518-546` (tree's own package.json scripts, `shell:true`) + `:1278-1306` (`npm ci|install` from `installToolFor`, no `--ignore-scripts`) → model-authored `postinstall`/`prepare` run at verification time, before any gate judged them. Fix: `--ignore-scripts` on the install leg; run declared scripts via resolved `.bin` argv (no shell) or deny lifecycle scripts; record the refusal in the verdict.
- **R11-f03 · P1 · "read-only" is the wrong label on `tree-run`.** CLI :1145-1205 + README-cli:184/:451/:465 sell it as "read-only, never re-emits" — true about writes, FALSE as an operator signal: it copies the dir to temp and **executes** its specs/entry/scripts. Fix (declare-or-strike): "never writes the tree; **does execute it** under the bounded runner (`sandbox:false`) — run only trees you trust" — same line on `check`/`repair --dir`/`emit --verify-kind`.
- **R11-f04 · P1 · the human-seat check is degenerate at both CLI sites (C2-f02 re-verified).** `:604-605` (approve) + `:1425-1426` (edit --write) pass `humanSeatToken` and `expectedHumanSeatToken` from the SAME env var → `policy.ts:171-180` collapses to "env var non-empty"; the unverified branch (`:237-240`) unreachable. **Failure path:** any shell with `RT_HUMAN_SEAT_TOKEN` exported mints `decidedBy:"human"` on a `forceHuman` gate. Fix: present via a separate channel; RED-first denied-on-leak test.
- **R11-f05 · P1 · `fixtures --approve` mints acceptance with no seat (A2-f01 re-verified).** `:977-998` stamps approval with no actor/token while README:22 promises human-gated; `policy.ts:156-180` refuses unverified seats by design. Fix: route through `evaluatePolicy` or stamp `decidedVia` + rename the claim.
- **R11-f06 · P2 · S-04 confirmed unfixed** (`browser-guard.mjs:34-48` no win32 branch → `C:\workspace\…` written; `BROWSER_ALLOW_EXTERNAL_HOST=1` disables the one intact guard). Fix: fail closed on win32 / physical paths.
- **R11-f07 · P3 · S-05 confirmed unfixed** (lexical `isInside` — `write-atomic.mjs`, **`write-set.ts:30-63` the emit writer**, CLI edit). Fix: realpath both dirs via one shared helper.
- **R11-f08 · P2 · `--force` keeps foreign files, then mints "tested" over a mixed tree (A1-f07 re-verified).** `headless.ts:1596-1607/1647` (`survivors`) → the tree-run judges bytes the factory never authored. Fix: quarantine (move aside, report) instead of keep-in-place.
- **R11-f09 · P2 · redaction covers two streams and two key shapes only.** Exact-value pass reads `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` alone (`node-runner.server.ts:230`) → `XAI_/TAVILY_API_KEY` never exact-matched; the `sk-` arm needs 40 chars total and misses a standard 32-char DeepSeek-shaped key from any non-env channel. Fix: iterate the registry's `apiKeyEnv` list; loosen the `sk-` arm; redact provider refusal text before it reaches packet/logs.
- **R11-f10 · P2 · the engine API accepts a machine `force` on a human-only gate.** `engine.ts:1650` blocks autopilot only when `!opts?.force`; reachable from `store.ts:1516-1551` (only a ≥12-char rationale) → mints `decidedBy:"human"` tokenless; `policy.ts:8-11`'s "no seat combination can bypass it" is true of the seam, not the callers. Fix: route `forceHitl` through `evaluatePolicy` inside store.approve.
- **R11-f11 · P2 · `.env` reaches `process.env` only via the TanStack load-env plugin** (Vite itself doesn't map it; read in node_modules) → README:45 true for the browser only via that plugin, false for the CLI. Fix: split the sentence per surface + a doc-claim test.
- **R11-f12 · P2 · the graders are still the trust anchor and don't grade the product (A3-f05).** A packet reader treats `acceptance.ok:true` as a working tree while a CJS-broken artifact passed. Fix: per-kind real graders + a `verification` label naming what was asserted.
- **R11-f13 · P3 · `--force`/valueless-flag asymmetry in emit** (A2-f11 re-verified).

### §R10 — THE 13-PHASE DATA FLOW (PARENT-COMPLETED from lane R10's own closed summary; append never landed. Traced through real bytes at HEAD e3d3fe4)
**The hop table (in→out→what breaks):** ①brief→create: `keyedDeepSeekConfigured` is a `process.env` read (**false in browser → console projects NEVER enforceDod** — A1-f09 cross-confirmed) · ③lattice: `attachAlgorithms` runs FIRST inside every `applyPhaseResult` → specGraph/contracts/oracle/crew **regenerated from the lattice every phase**, overwriting ingested design content (R10-f10) · ⑤codegen: **`project.contracts` is NEVER read by codegen.server.ts** (zero grep hits), `project.oracle` neither — the LLM writes module bodies blind to the A6 contract and A7 oracle the pipeline advertises (R10-f01) · ⑥emit: verbatim push (R10-f07, A3-f01 at the hop) · ⑦tree-run: CLI `tree-run` never calls `h.saveProject` → verdict never reaches `treeRunTrail` (R10-f08) · ⑧verify: evidence gate is `specs>0 && assertions>0` (the hollow-tree hole); partial keeps the floor's green lastTest (R10-f02/f03/f04) · ⑩handover: serves the STORED string; runAcceptance/ingestResult never re-stamp (R10-f06). **Keyed vs keyless diverge at hop 5 and never converge** — and the CLI `run`/`gate` cross-confirms A1-f02: the mint is reachable only from `driveP6Journey`/`repairTreeDir`, which no CLI verb calls.
- **R10-f01 · P1** contracts never reach codegen (spec: thread entities/endpoints into the prompt builders; done-when: the contract entity name appears in emitted tree code). · **R10-f02 · P2** packet drops `decidedVia` on both faces (spec: additive `decided_via` on envelope rows + prose). · **R10-f03 · P2** rail mislabels a tree-run mint as "P6 template self-test" (`packet.ts:605` unconditional on label). · **R10-f04 · P2** `capabilities.verify:"config-gated"` is a hardcoded literal (`:125`). · **R10-f05 · P2** `model_route` names a model that served no phase content. · **R10-f06 · P2** runAcceptance/ingest never re-stamp the packet → a handed-over packet can predate the verdict it describes. · **R10-f07 · P1** emit-time materialization absent (A3-f01). · **R10-f08 · P2** `tree-run --project` writes no ledger row (red hand-runs leave no trail). · **R10-f09 · P2** the SERVED model id is threaded into provenance (T5.4) but `modelRoute` is a hardcoded constant — the two honesty surfaces disagree about the same call. · **R10-f10 · P3** design hop carries nothing forward (regeneration overwrites). · **R10-f11 · P3** `bareNodeLiveOverlay` mints non-deterministic artifact ids (`research-${phase}-${i}-${now}`) vs the engine's deterministic ids → same run, different packet bytes across live paths (breaks byte-idempotence). Wiring specs (8, each file:line → change → done-when) in the lane summary; stop points named.

### §R12 — PERFORMANCE · RELIABILITY ON THE 8 GB BOX (PARENT-COMPLETED from lane R12's own closed summary; append never landed)
- **R12-f01 · P1 · O(chunks²) prompt growth.** `codegen.server.ts:3053/3117/3078` re-sends EVERY prior file line in EVERY later chunk (`prior` unbounded; plan one chunk per ≤8 sigs) + full spec lists per chunk → N/8 sequential calls, k-th carrying Σ(k−1) lines, each with a 240s timeout. Fix: cap prior rendering (`P6_SYNTH_PRIOR_MAX_FILES`≈12 + a visible elision marker) + a per-chunk byte budget. Done-when: 200-prior unit test asserts ≤budget AND the marker present.
- **R12-f02 · P1 · `RAPIDTOOL_TOKEN_CAP` cannot stop a runaway (per-call ledger).** `synthWithBackoff(:2315)` defaults `emptyLedger()`; both production sites pass no ledger → the cap only trips within ONE call. Fix: thread one per-run ledger. Done-when: cap=100, three sub-cap calls → call #2 returns `RT_E_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED`. (Cross-ref A2-f07.)
- **R12-f03 · P1 · treeRun deep-copies `node_modules` into %TEMP%.** `compile-check.server.ts:946-948` `cpSync` **no filter** (contrast `:1458` which filters) → duplicated dep trees on the 8GB box; the `finally` rmSync silently swallows Windows locks → temp dirs accumulate. Fix: `:1458`-style filter or a hardlink seam; if deps aren't copied, emit the existing UNVERIFIED skip note (never a fabricated pass).
- **R12-f04 · P1 · the watchdog step budget is SMALLER than a legal step.** `WILD_STEP_BUDGET_MS = 90_000` vs `P6_SYNTH_TIMEOUT_MS = 240_000` → a legitimate keyed step auto-pauses as "frozen". **Latent-blocking: becomes live the day A1-f01 is fixed.** Fix: derive the P6 budget from the phase's own max. Done-when: a >180s step does not set the stall latch.
- **R12-f05 · P2** stale-run telemetry defeats its coalescer (changing `stale Ns` in the messageHash → a localStorage write every ~11s). Fix: stable message + seconds in payload. · **R12-f06 · P2** `readRtTail` reads the WHOLE file to keep 256KB (`readFileSync` then slice — 3× alloc per dir). Fix: true tail read. · **R12-f07 · P2** `diagnose`'s growth surface is hardcoded zeros — the detector is inert (`projectFile: 0` always; measured 158,302 B counter-example). Fix: `statSync` + real counts. · **R12-f08 · P2** `rebaseConcurrentEngine` appends artifacts/decisions/memory/research with NO caps (logs capped beside them) → unbounded persist blob. Fix: named caps + include in `salvageTrim`. · **R12-f09 · P2** the whole project set is serialized synchronously on every persisted `set` (no debounce) → mainthread jank the watchdog then reports as its own stall. Fix: trailing debounce + the existing crash-flush seam. · **R12-f10 · P2/P3** the run-marker crash path OVERWRITES the prior `results.json` while the marker still says unfinished (`run-eval.mjs:2788-2799` unconditional catch write) → the file is neither the prior run nor a real result. Fix: never clobber on crash — write `results.json.crashed-<ts>` or `marker.crash`. · **R12-f11 · P3** `IMPORT_MAX_CHARS = 10MB` exceeds the ~5MB storage it must survive. Fix: lower or return `tooLargeToPersist`.
**R12 verified-clean (do not re-audit):** node-runner.server.ts settle guarantees/treeKill/caps/redact-order (486 lines read) · rt-ring bounded/corrupt-safe · rt-client WeakSet + idempotent install · **console.tsx: 0 useEffect listener/interval registrations without cleanup** (17 sites) · watchdog timers always cleared in finally · store H4 layer coherent · asyncChains self-delete/100-entry LRU/failureMemo TTL'd. **OOM/starve ranking (the 8GB wall):** ①treeRun node_modules copy ②readRtTail whole-file ×N ③32k-token responses + O(chunks²) prompts ④un-debounced serialize + uncapped rebase.

### AW45 · CORRECTION + NEW FINDINGS (the 331 wave, 2026-10-02) — the keyed path is NOT funding-blocked

**AW45-c01 · P0 · `O-1` ("Fund the DeepSeek account — all keyed calls 402") is FALSIFIED.** Live today: `GET https://api.deepseek.com/models` with the repo `.env` key → **200**; `POST /chat/completions` `deepseek-v4-flash` and `deepseek-v4-pro` → **200** with real completions and billed usage. `deepseek-v4-flash` is accepted on the request path and served as `deepseek-flash`; the ids in `deepseek.ts` are correct. The register's "account unfunded → all keyed done-whens blocked" premise has therefore been **false for an unknown number of waves**, and every `O-1`/`O-4` deferral written against it should be re-read. Root cause of the misdiagnosis: `DEEPSEEK_API_KEY` was set at **Windows User scope** with an invalid value (tail `…42ff`), while nothing in `src/`, `scripts/`, or `.grok/` loaded `.env` (`grep -rn 'loadEnvFile|dotenv'` → 0 hits) — so the CLI sent the stale ambient key and every keyed call 401'd. Fixed in this wave by lane 331-1 (`src/lib/llm/providers/key-resolution.ts`, `scripts/rapidtool-cli.mjs`): the repo `.env` key wins, a differing non-blank ambient value raises the coded `RT_E_LLM_KEY_SHADOWED` naming both last-4 tails, and a 401 now classifies as `RT_E_LLM_KEY_REJECTED` (with the source named) instead of `RT_E_UNKNOWN`. **Owner action remains, and it is not code:** clearing the stale User-scope variable silences the signal (deliberately left in place — other tools on the host may read it).

**AW45-c02 · P0 · the keyed run works end-to-end and the M-A substance bar is MET — the remaining gap is two generator defects.** First funded keyed run recorded here: brief prose → `create` → keyed `gate`/`approve`/`gate` → P6. Result: `lattice.jobs = ["flip","invoice","track","rehab","bid"]`, and P6 keyed synthesis emitted **7 real modules** (`create-property.ts` 14,755 B, `list-line-items.ts` 2,760 B, `validate-line-item-input.ts` 9,659 B, `bid.ts` 5,507 B, `invoice.ts` 16,888 B, `flip.ts` 27,090 B, plus the `src/generated.ts` barrel), with `p6-arch: PASS` and `p6-brief-fit` PASS. `treeRun.substance = {stubs:0, reexports:0, real:3}` — the `{0,0,≥3}` bar R6-f05/W-12 measured as unmet **is met on a keyed run**. Full evidence: `wave_plans/W45/parent-keyed-findings.md`.

**AW45-c03 · P0 · cross-chunk duplicate declaration ships an unloadable module.** `src/jobs/flip.ts` line 2 imports `estimateARV` from `../generated/list-line-items.ts` **and** line 546 declares `async function estimateARV(propertyId)` → `SyntaxError: Identifier 'estimateARV' has already been declared` on load. `create-property.ts` and `list-line-items.ts` both also export `validatePropertyInput`. This is the W5-L1 chunk-order tension (fixes/216k) reaching production. Owner: lane 331-4 (validator/prompt side, `codegen.server.ts`).

**AW45-c04 · P0 · `proseLineHits` (`codegen.server.ts:355`) has two live false positives that refuse valid code.** Direct call of the exported function: `return a.index - b.index;` → flagged (the bare-arithmetic form `balance: amountDue - amountPaid` is correctly exempt), and `arvNote: arvAvailable ? undefined : 'ARV unavailable: no comparables',` → flagged (`return negative ? -cents : cents;` is correctly exempt). Real consequence: `repair`'s own patched output for `invoice.ts` and `list-line-items.ts` was refused as "still fails validation — prose-in-code", which blocked the self-repair loop. Third and fourth instance of the class already recorded in the same docblock (W23-262/B1, 322 X3/BUG-4). Owner: lane 331-4.

**AW45-c05 · P0 · `repair` cannot target the file named in a load failure.** `repair --budget 3` burned all three cycles at the identical error (`flip.ts:546`) while `flip.ts` appeared in neither `applied.written` nor `patchRefusals` — the repair directive does not include the entry file of a *load* failure (only the per-spec test-failure shape). Separately, `src/generated.ts` — the path the emitter itself writes as `TREE_ENTRY_PATH` — was refused by both the import and apply sides as "outside the scaffold's src/ subdirs and tests/", so the tree's own barrel cannot round-trip. Owner: lane 331-3 (`headless.ts` repairTreeDir / the emitted-tree allowlist).

**AW45-c06 · P1 · `summary.budget.spent` resets per invocation.** Observed across three successive `gate` calls on one project: `446` → `0` → `77654`. Live confirmation of `R12-f02` (the cap cannot stop a runaway because the ledger is per-call), and a second reason the operator-facing spend line under-reports a run.

**AW45-c07 · P2 · `create --brief` silently accepts a file path as the brief.** `create --brief research/eval-corpus/house-flip-manager.md` builds a project whose brief *is* that literal string; `inferName` then names it after the path and the lattice extracts "flip" from "house-flip-manager". The corpus fixtures are front-matter documents (`name`, `expectedNouns`, `expectedCapabilities`) that only `brief-fixtures.ts` parses. A plausible invocation that silently produces a nonsense project is worth a coded refusal or an `@file` form. Owner: lane 331-1 (report-only this wave).

**AW45-c08 · (struck) three "silent catches" in `src/lib/auth/popup.server.ts:163-173` are NOT defects.** They sit inside an inline browser `<script>` template string and are documented best-effort handlers whose failure path is a user-visible "sign-in was cancelled or failed", never a silent accept. Recorded so the next sweep does not re-report them.

**Method note for the next wave:** the 331 wave ran six parallel lanes under the standing disjoint-ownership rule and then closed the boundary. Lane landing logs: `fixes/331_1_keywright.md` … `fixes/331_6_warden.md`; wave contract and lane briefs: `wave_plans/W45/`.
